The key ideas are why nonlocalized queries allow cumulative history to create coherent gains, and how the finite-dimensional score law reconciles larger support with better recoverability. After that, read the Transformer mechanism section, which is where the abstract result becomes relevant to actual model design through attention and residual writing.

If time is limited, read the introduction, the three state definitions, the main theorem and its figures, the minimax result for weighted superpositions, and the GPT-2 or token-level experiments. About 30 to 45 minutes should be enough for the main line. The deeper questions are whether the query class matches real next-step computation in LLM reasoning, how sensitive the conclusions are to the independent Gaussian embedding assumption, and how one would train a recurrent or latent-CoT model to preserve history in a balanced way without flooding the state with irrelevant information.72:T430,The authors build three prompt sets. Dataset A contains self-generated text filtered for prompts that induce echoing. Dataset B is created through recursive prompting: a model answer is fed back as the next fresh user prompt, producing inputs that resemble assistant-style text rather than ordinary user requests. Dataset C contains non-echoing prompts from a similar generation pipeline. Echo labels use a strict prefix-only rule: full echo requires the completion to begin with the complete prompt token sequence, while partial echo requires the beginning to copy at least 40% of the prompt tokens. The mechanism tests include weak overlap search in public training mixtures such as SmolTalk and Tulu-3 SFT, forced-prefix experiments where the answer is seeded with the first 2, 3, or 5 prompt tokens, and ablation of high-scoring induction/copying heads ranked by the prefix-matching score from Olsson et al. Head ablation is implemented by zeroing selected attention-output slices during generation and comparing top-copying-head ablation against random-head controls.73:T751,The study analyzes three open instruction-tuned models: Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct, and Mistral-7B-Instruct. Data come from filtered EPITOME peer-support dialogues, with short seeker posts and self-harm-related cases removed to reduce context sparsity and safety-template confounds. For each empathy mechanism, the authors build contrastive sets and extract steering vectors from residual-stream activations.

The main intervention is contrastive activation addition: a direction is estimated from activation differences between higher-scoring and lower-scoring examples for a given mechanism, then added at inference time to a chosen layer. The contribution is less about inventing a new steering algorithm and more about the analysis stack built around it.

There are three key components. First, a layer sweep identifies where interventions are stable across models; middle layers emerge as the most reliable, with layer 15 receiving special attention in the analysis. Second, target versus off-target evaluation measures not only whether ER steering raises ER, but also whether it unintentionally shifts IP and EX, and likewise for the other directions. This tests separability rather than raw controllability. Third, geometric and projection analyses examine pairwise structure among ER/IP/EX directions and quantify how much persona-induced activation movement lies inside the recovered mechanism subspace.

Compared with prior activation-steering work on honesty, refusal, or sycophancy, the difference is substantial. The target behavior here is inherently multi-component. The authors use steering as a representational probe, not just a control knob. And they explicitly test whether prompt-induced persona effects align with the recovered mechanism basis, which is a deeper question than simply comparing response scores under different personas.74:T700,The experiments support three main findings. First, steering is feasible. Across all three models, contrastive activation addition at middle layers consistently shifts EPITOME proxy scores in the intended direction. The trend survives evaluation with a second automated judge, which reduces the chance that the result is only an artifact of one classifier. The paper also reports partial out-of-domain transfer, suggesting the vectors are not purely dataset-specific surface heuristics.

Second, the three empathy mechanisms are not independently controllable. Steering along one recovered direction improves the target mechanism, but also causes measurable off-target changes in the other two. The result is not that ER, IP, and EX are indistinguishable; rather, they are distinguishable but share representational structure strongly enough that clean disentanglement fails. This is the central empirical basis for the paper’s “multi-axial” claim.

Third, persona effects mostly fall outside the recovered mechanism basis. Persona prompts can produce large changes in output-level empathy scores, yet the corresponding activation shifts project only weakly onto the ER/IP/EX subspace. The headline number is that at layer 15, the recovered ER/IP/EX basis explains only about 3% of the persona-induced mean-squared activation-shift magnitude. That is a strong indication that persona-conditioned empathy variation is not just movement along “more ER,” “more IP,” or “more EX,” but involves broader representational changes.

All experiments are on 7B–8B instruction-tuned models. Evaluation relies mainly on EPITOME classifiers plus an LLM judge, so the findings are best read as robust mechanism-level evidence rather than a final statement about human-perceived empathy.75:T48c,For a skim, start with the two research questions in the introduction, then jump to the method figure and the layer-sweep results. Three parts deserve attention.

First, the vector construction and layer sweep. This shows how the ER/IP/EX contrastive sets are built and why middle-layer interventions are more stable than shallow or late-layer edits. It helps distinguish semantic steering from near-logit perturbation.

Second, the spillover results across mechanisms. This is the core evidence in the paper. Do not only look at target gains; inspect how much the non-target dimensions move and whether the pattern is consistent across models.

Third, the persona projection analysis. The small projection ratio at layer 15 is the most informative number in the paper. It raises the right follow-up question: is the EPITOME basis too narrow, or do persona prompts act through other channels such as style, identity alignment, politeness, or safety-related templates?

A focused read should take about 25–35 minutes. If time is tight, the method overview, layer sweep, spillover matrix, and persona projection figures are enough to capture the main contribution.76:T565,The method decomposes any additive update Δ with respect to a reference vector z into orthogonal components: Δ=Δ_parallel+Δ_perp. In residual space, Δ is the sub-layer residual update and z is the sub-layer input (or block input for block-level updates). In attention value space, Δ is the pre-output-projection value aggregation, decomposed relative to the token’s own value vector; this separates self-value flow from cross-token aggregation effects.

Causality is tested via component-scaling interventions: multiply either Δ_parallel or Δ_perp by a scalar and measure behavioral degradation. The key empirical asymmetry is consistent across models and layers: scaling Δ_perp is highly disruptive, while scaling Δ_parallel is comparatively benign over a wide range—especially in value space for cross-token aggregation.

The paper then extends the geometry to two applied settings:
- Compression: decompose quantization/pruning-induced update distortion into parallel vs perpendicular error; perpendicular error is more predictive of final quality.
- Pretraining: introduce parallel attention suppression (residual-space and value-space variants). The motivation is to discourage learning direction-preserving attention components that appear behaviorally redundant, potentially reallocating capacity toward direction-changing updates and stabilizing optimization.77:Ta28,文章的技术路线是把每一种失败模式都拆成四步：先展示最初看起来像发现的现象，再指出暴露问题的 tell，再给出修复协议，最后用独立控制验证修复是否有效。贯穿全文的主线是四条纪律：第一，用 positive-control ladder 校准仪器，确认它在目标位置真的有区分能力；第二，用 orthogonal cell 做认证，避免同一类测量自证；第三，在花算力做 patch 或大规模干预前先算 power；第四，所有“reads X”结论都必须相对模型 commit 深度来陈述。

文中一个关键背景是 decision site 的低维瓶颈。作者用 participation ratio 估计残差流协方差的有效维度，发现四个模型在控制 token 处只有约 8 到 15 维，而同层内容 token 位置仍是 33 到 40+ 的健康高维。这个事实解释了为什么 projection-fraction instrument 会失灵：在一个只有十来个有效自由度的通道里，正控方向投到自身 span 的比例可能并不比随机方向高，导致“低投影”不能说明缺失，只能说明仪器没分辨率。

六个 failure mode 里，较有方法学含金量的几个是：
1. 对 projection fraction，作者要求先做 band/below-null 检查和正控梯度，只有当正控在该位置能明显高于 null，投影读数才可解释。
2. 对 covariance-matched null，作者指出在 massive-activation family 中，匹配协方差会让几乎所有方向都显得“典型”，null 失去对比度，因此需要额外的 family-aware 控制，而不是只看协方差匹配。
3. 对 per-head OV attribution，作者发现 reordered-normalization 架构里按 head 估计的写入量会比真实 residual write 高约三倍，因此不能把 OV 路径贡献直接当作残差写入，需要用真实残差差分或更接近执行图的估计来校正。
4. 对 interchange patch，作者强调如果 outcome 已经贴近 ceiling，engage/disengage 两个方向会因为 operating point 不对称而出现 sign-chaotic；修复方式是把结论写成 depth- and operating-point-referenced，并在非饱和区间做验证。
5. 对 read-from verdict，作者要求把测量层和 commit 层分开：如果模型在更早层已经决定，晚层读到的相关性可能只是决策后的回声，而不是因果读取。

整体上，这不是提出单一新算法，而是把 interpretability instrument 当成实验仪器来做校准学。它和很多 mechanistic interpretability 文章最大的区别，是作者不急着给机制故事，而是先给失效边界和可复现 protocol。78:Tb2f,The paper is organized instrument by instrument. For each failure mode, it follows the same pattern: show the initial apparent finding, identify the tell that exposed the failure, propose a repair protocol, and certify the repair with an orthogonal control. Four procedural rules run through the whole note: calibrate against a positive-control ladder, certify with an orthogonal cell, compute statistical power before spending compute on interventions, and state every read-from claim relative to the model’s commitment depth.

A key empirical backdrop is the low-dimensional decision-site bottleneck. The authors estimate effective dimensionality with the participation ratio of the residual-stream covariance and find that the control token sits in an 8-to-15 dimensional channel across four models, while content positions at comparable layers remain much higher-rank. This observation explains one of the main instrument failures: in such a narrow channel, a projection-fraction metric can lose discriminative power exactly where the interesting directions live, so a low projection score may reflect instrument weakness rather than representational absence.

Among the six failure modes, several stand out as broadly reusable methodology:
1. For projection-based instruments, the protocol requires checking whether the positive control is actually above the null band at the target position. If the instrument cannot separate its own positive control from null, the projection number is not interpretable.
2. For covariance-matched nulls, the paper shows that in massive-activation families the null can saturate until nearly every direction looks typical. Matching covariance alone is therefore insufficient; one needs family-aware controls or additional diagnostics.
3. For per-head OV attribution, the authors show that reordered-normalization architectures can induce about a 3x overestimate relative to the true residual write. The fix is to ground attribution in actual residual differences or a closer execution-level estimator, rather than treating OV-path attribution as the write itself.
4. For interchange patching, they show that operating-point asymmetry matters. When one arm is near ceiling, engage/disengage interventions can become sign-chaotic. The remedy is to report effects relative to operating point and verify them away from saturation.
5. For read-from claims, the paper separates the read layer from the commitment layer. If the model has already committed earlier, a later-layer correlation may be a post-decision echo rather than a causal read.

The paper’s distinctive move is to treat interpretability tools as laboratory instruments that require calibration, power analysis, and certification. It is much less interested in a mechanism narrative than in the validity conditions under which such a narrative can be trusted.79:T5bd,The most important quantitative result is the decision-site bottleneck itself. The participation ratio at the control token is reported as 14.7 for OLMo-3-7B-Instruct, 8.6 for Qwen2.5-7B, 10.2 for Llama-3.1-8B, and roughly 9.4 raw / 12.8 standardized for GPT-OSS-20B at its relevant decision token. These values are only 4% to 8% of their column-shuffle references, indicating a strong compression rather than a mild reduction in rank. By contrast, content positions at similar layers remain in the 33+ to 40+ range, supporting the claim that the bottleneck is localized to the decision site.

The paper is not benchmark-driven. Its empirical contribution is a set of quantified diagnostic failures. The headline numbers matter because they are large enough to reverse conclusions: per-head OV attribution can overshoot true residual writes by about threefold on reordered-norm models; covariance-matched nulls can saturate so badly that every direction appears typical; read-from verdicts can become artifacts when measured after commitment. These are not small calibration errors but systematic distortions.

Another useful aspect of the experimental presentation is that the authors do not overclaim universality. Coverage differs by failure mode, and not every protocol is established on all four models. That restraint is a strength: the note presents portable procedures tested within a concrete program, not a blanket claim that every failure appears everywhere.7a:T8ae,论文沿用 datamodeling 设定：用连续权重 w 给每个训练样本加权，训练算法 A（含优化器、LR schedule、随机性固定）把 w 映射到最终参数 θ(w)，每个测试查询 q 的标量测量 φ_q(θ)（常见是 loss）与训练权重的导数 ∂f_q/∂w_i 就是“样本 i 对查询 q 的影响”。把所有查询与所有训练样本的影响堆成影响矩阵（Jacobian）J∈R^{k×n}。

核心重述是：MAGIC 能给出 J 的某一行（固定 q，对所有训练样本的影响）或某些向量-雅可比积/雅可比-向量积，但若要对每个 q 都跑一次反传穿越训练轨迹，成本随 k 线性爆炸。作者把“预算受限的多查询归因”表述为：在只能获得少量对 J 的线性测量（left/right vector products）的情况下，估计整个大矩阵。

论文区分了两个目标，并指出它们对应不同的最优测量策略：
1) reconstruction：尽量在 Frobenius 意义下重建 J（更偏向把大行范数的查询重建得更好）；
2) attribution：更像“每个查询一票”的平均归因质量（不希望被少数大范数查询主导）。作者强调这两者的差异不是表述细节，而会改变你应该如何分配测量预算。

在算法上给出两条路线，均建立在现有 metagradient 原语之上：
- Mage（Metagradient Approximation by Gram Eigendirections）：面向 reconstruction。直觉是用少量测量去捕捉影响矩阵的主能量子空间（类似从 Gram 矩阵的特征方向提取主成分），让重建误差主要落在尾部方向。
- Spell（Spherical Estimation for the Linear datamodeling score at Low budgets）：面向 attribution。它更强调对查询方向的“均匀覆盖”，用球面/各向同性的测量设计减少对行范数分布的偏置，从而在低预算下让“平均查询”的归因更稳。

与以往可扩展归因（近似 influence function、tracing、Shapley 近似）相比，这篇工作的关键差别不在于再造一个新的近似公式，而是：以 MAGIC 的精确 metagradient 为底座，把问题提升为“矩阵估计 + 测量设计”，并明确区分‘重建矩阵’与‘做归因评分’两种评价口径。7b:T80a,They adopt the datamodeling setup: assign a continuous weight vector w over training points, run a fixed (randomness-fixed) training algorithm A to get parameters θ(w), and define each test query q as a scalar measurement φ_q(θ) (typically loss). The influence scores are the Jacobian entries ∂f_q/∂w_i, and stacking them yields a large influence matrix J ∈ R^{k×n} (k queries, n training points).

MAGIC-style exact influence can compute influences accurately by differentiating through the entire training trajectory, but doing this per query scales linearly in k. The key reframing is: multi-query attribution becomes an influence-matrix estimation problem from a limited number of linear measurements (left/right vector products obtainable via existing metagradient machinery).

A central conceptual contribution is separating two objectives:
- Reconstruction: recover J well in a global (Frobenius-like) sense, which implicitly prioritizes high-row-norm queries.
- Attribution: optimize attribution quality per query more uniformly, avoiding domination by a few large-norm rows.
They show these objectives imply different optimal measurement designs.

They then propose two estimators built on metagradient primitives:
- Mage (Metagradient Approximation by Gram Eigendirections) targets reconstruction by focusing measurements along dominant eigendirections of an associated Gram structure, effectively capturing the high-energy subspace of J.
- Spell (Spherical Estimation for the Linear datamodeling score at Low budgets) targets attribution by using more isotropic/spherical measurement strategies to improve average-per-query attribution under low budgets.

Compared to prior scalable attribution (approximate influence functions, tracing, Shapley approximations), the novelty is less about a new heuristic approximation and more about (i) treating exact metagradients as measurements, (ii) casting the task as matrix estimation, and (iii) making the reconstruction-vs-attribution distinction operational via different measurement schemes.7c:T92e,方法结构很清楚：冻结 actor π_actor，训练 coach π_θ。先让 actor 在输入 x 上生成初始轨迹 y；coach 读取 (x, y)，输出一段自然语言经验 e；然后把 e 作为额外条件，再让同一个 actor 在目标样本 z 上作答，并用 verifier 判断 guided response 是否正确，把这个结果作为 coach 的奖励。关键点有三层。

第一层是奖励设计。same-instance reward 令 z=x，鼓励 coach 做错误诊断、保留有用中间结果、删掉误导性推理；cross-instance reward 则让 z 来自与 x 不同但同任务分布的样本集合 P，迫使 coach 提炼更抽象的任务规则，而不是只写针对单题的补丁。这个区分很重要，因为它把“经验知识”从 case-specific critique 和 transferable strategy 两种形态上拆开了。

第二层是优化方式。作者不是对单个 e 做 REINFORCE，而是对每个源轨迹采样多个候选经验片段，经过 actor rollout 得到组内相对优劣，再用 GRPO 训练 coach。这样做的原因很直接：经验文本质量很难做 token-level 监督，且 reward 只能通过 actor 的最终表现间接观测；组相对优化比单样本高方差策略梯度更稳。

第三层是多轮经验更新。初始 e^(0)=∅，第 k 轮 coach 根据 actor 最新轨迹和已有经验生成更新后的 e^(k)，再让 actor 继续解题。这里不是简单把历史越堆越长，而是“替换式更新”经验表示，让 coach 有机会删除前一轮错误结论、保留已验证信息、补充新发现。这个设计比 naive memory accumulation 更合理，因为原始轨迹里错误会累积，替换式摘要更接近 belief state 更新。

和 self-refinement 的区别在于：self-refinement 直接把原模型的长轨迹或自我批评再喂回原模型，提炼能力仍由 actor 自己承担；L2C 把这件事外包给专门训练过的 coach。和直接 RL 微调 actor 的区别在于：L2C 不改 actor 参数，因此不会轻易破坏 actor 的通用能力，也更适合服务多个冻结 actor 或部署后持续收集经验的场景。论文里还分析了 coach 的 actor-specific 性质：同一个 coach 对不同 actor 的帮助并不完全可互换，说明它学到的不是抽象“正确 critique”，而是“什么样的提示最能让这个 actor 改正”。7d:T8d7,The setup is simple and well scoped. A frozen actor π_actor first produces a trajectory y on input x. A trainable coach π_θ reads (x, y) and outputs a natural-language experience snippet e. The same frozen actor is then prompted with e on a target instance z, and a deterministic verifier scores whether the guided response is correct. That score becomes the reward for the coach.

There are three important design choices.

First, reward variants. With same-instance reward, z=x, so the coach is encouraged to diagnose what went wrong on that specific example and preserve useful intermediate results. With cross-instance reward, z is sampled from a disjoint set of related instances, forcing the coach to extract reusable structure such as task rules or general strategies. This distinction is central: it separates local error correction from transferable experience.

Second, optimization. For each source trajectory, the coach samples multiple candidate experience snippets, each is evaluated through actor rollouts, and the coach is trained with GRPO using relative outcomes within the group. This makes sense because there is no token-level supervision for “good experience,” and the reward is only observable through the actor’s downstream behavior.

Third, iterative experience updating. Starting from empty experience, the coach repeatedly rewrites the current experience after each new actor attempt. The updated experience replaces the previous one rather than simply appending more history. That matters because raw trajectories accumulate errors and irrelevant details; replacement makes the experience representation closer to a belief-state update than to naive memory growth.

Compared with self-refinement, the key difference is division of labor. In self-refinement, the actor must also do the extraction and filtering of its own trajectory. In L2C, that filtering is delegated to a separately trained model. Compared with RL-tuning the actor, L2C preserves the actor’s parameters and broad capabilities, which is attractive for frozen-model deployment settings. The paper also argues that the learned coach is actor-specific: it learns not just what is correct, but what kind of guidance is most effective for that particular actor.7e:T688,The experiments cover mathematical reasoning and interactive text games, which is a sensible pairing: math tests error diagnosis and reuse of intermediate results, while text games test whether the coach can extract latent environment rules from long, noisy interaction histories. Across tasks and model sizes, L2C outperforms the frozen actor, self-refinement, and an untrained coach.

Two findings stand out. First, gains are larger in text games. That is consistent with the method’s premise: when trajectories contain hidden rules and long-horizon evidence, a trained coach has more room to compress useful structure and discard noise. The cross-instance reward is especially helpful when different instances share underlying rules, because it pressures the coach to learn reusable guidance rather than case-specific patches.

Second, iterative coaching behaves like a form of test-time scaling. Performance keeps improving over more iterations with a trained coach, while an untrained coach quickly saturates. On math tasks, allocating extra compute to coach-guided iterations beats simply doubling the actor’s decoding budget. That is an important systems-level result: not all extra inference compute is equally useful, and structured experience extraction can dominate brute-force decoding.

The paper also includes analyses of reward variants, out-of-distribution transfer, and actor-specific transfer. The provided text does not include the full result tables, so it is better not to invent exact numbers, but the evidence structure is clear: task-level comparisons, iteration scaling curves, same-instance vs cross-instance ablations, and transfer analyses.7f:T462,Realtime-Venus contains two separately trained 9B frontend models built on MiniCPM-o 4.5: Realtime-Venus-Omni for audio-visual interaction and Realtime-Venus-Audio for speech-only interaction. The frontend jointly predicts control tokens, response text, and private delegation requests in a streaming sequence. This lets the model learn when to keep speaking through a backchannel, when to stop after a real interruption, when to revise an unspoken continuation, and when to launch background work. The runtime uses a dual-loop design. The conversational model handles live interaction, while Realtime-Venus-Harness executes registered capabilities asynchronously. Each delegation is bound to its original session and committed with the evidence available at the request boundary. Returned results are inserted as private context only after freshness and playback-aware delivery checks, leaving the frontend in charge of the user-facing response. The data pipeline combines scenario planning, speech realization, and temporal alignment, with trajectories for duplex behavior, proactive responses, and delegation workflows.80:T40a,For pretraining-oriented readers, the main contribution is the training signal design rather than the leaderboard outcome. The Verifiable Experience Pipeline ties tasks, trajectories, tool calls, generated artifacts, and external verification evidence into a single training unit. That is meaningfully different from plain SFT on traces, from RLHF-style preference tuning, and from synthetic agent data that lacks a grounded success criterion. The useful takeaway is threefold. First, it pushes agent training closer to a mid-training or continual-training regime instead of treating tool use as a thin post-training layer. Second, it treats failures as a source for new task construction and environment refinement, which connects naturally to curriculum design, active data construction, and continual pretraining. Third, it studies the development of the model itself as an empirical case of human-agent collaboration. The contribution is better read as a systems-and-paradigm paper than as a fully decomposed, reproducible recipe.81:T960,The model is built on top of a 744B-parameter MoE foundation model, but the technical center of gravity is the experience pipeline rather than the base architecture.

Step one is grounding each training task in a real execution environment. Instead of only seeing an instruction and a target answer, the model observes environment state, calls tools, inspects outputs, produces intermediate artifacts, and revises its behavior based on feedback. Final outcomes are checked through domain-specific external signals such as executable tests, experiment metrics, file state, geometric checks, source support, or human-defined criteria. This moves supervision away from surface-form imitation toward world-grounded task completion.

Step two is recording a richer training object. A sample is not just prompt-response; it is task + trajectory + artifacts + verification evidence. This matters because it preserves the behavioral chain and the basis for judging success, which should help the model learn when to inspect state, when to invoke tools, and when to recover from failure.

Step three is trajectory curation. The pipeline removes incomplete, contradictory, duplicate, or behaviorally invalid examples. The last category is the interesting one: a trace may look linguistically plausible but still be inconsistent with environment state, produced artifacts, or verification evidence. That is a stronger filter than ordinary text deduplication or heuristic trace cleaning.

Step four is failure-driven task construction. Recurrent failure modes such as poor tool choice, incomplete verification, missing evidence, and failed recovery are used to create new tasks and quality checks. For longer workflows, the recorded experience includes launching jobs, inspecting logs and artifacts, and revising execution plans. The intended effect is to teach transferable debugging and recovery behavior rather than narrow task scripts.

Methodologically, this is not a new RL algorithm and not standard outcome-reward optimization. It is closer to a data engine anchored by external verification, sitting somewhere between agentic mid-training and post-training. Compared with pure outcome supervision, it keeps process information. Compared with pure process distillation, it constrains the process with external results. Compared with online RL, it emphasizes experience construction, filtering, and reuse.82:T804,The empirical section has two parts: capability evaluation on 16 agent benchmarks, and an analysis of the human-agent workflow during the development of Atria Dawn itself.

On benchmark performance, the paper reports evaluation across 16 benchmarks covering tool use, search/research, workspace productivity, professional tasks, software engineering, terminal tasks, ML engineering, and cybersecurity. Atria Dawn Preview is reported as the top score on five benchmarks and second on three more. The currently visible text is more of a results overview than a fully unpacked academic evaluation, so it is difficult to attribute gains cleanly to the base model, the experience pipeline, the tool interfaces, or benchmark protocol differences. One useful signal is that the authors explicitly note uneven performance across domains, which suggests the system is not uniformly strong across all agent settings.

The more informative evidence is the development-process analysis. The paper uses 769 task records from 56 participants, combined with agent logs, to study role allocation. A notable number is that roughly one-third of completed AI-assisted tasks were rated by participants as infeasible without AI under the same scope and resource constraints. The analysis also separates finer roles such as proposing a method, selecting among proposals, diagnosing a difficulty, and executing a revision. The reported pattern is that agents often initiate approaches and carry out changes, while humans concentrate on evaluation, selection, and steering. That is not a standard benchmark result, but it is highly relevant for anyone thinking about recursive improvement loops and agent-assisted model development.

The main empirical limitation is the lack of deeper decomposition in the visible material: data volume, number of experience units, success/failure mix, contribution of different verification channels, and ablations on curation rules are not yet sufficiently exposed. Those details would be necessary to turn the paper into a reusable recipe.83:T559,Read this paper with a training-signal lens rather than a benchmark lens.

Start with the introduction and the Atria Dawn Preview section. The key question is what exactly counts as supervision. The important object is not the 744B MoE base model, but the binding of task, trajectory, artifact, and verification evidence.

Then focus on the Verifiable Experience Pipeline. Three details matter most: what kinds of environment feedback are treated as supervision, what makes an example behaviorally invalid, and how failure cases are converted into new tasks and checks. Those points determine whether this is merely careful data engineering or a more general training methodology.

After that, inspect the benchmark table and case studies, but do not over-index on rank. Pay more attention to task coverage, which tasks require long-horizon recovery, which rely on evidence verification, and whether the paper distinguishes single-step tool use from multi-stage workflows.

Finally, read the analysis of the 769 development records. The role taxonomy, the way feasibility is judged, and the evidence behind the “agent proposes, human selects” pattern are the most useful parts if you care about self-improvement loops.

A quick skim takes about 30-40 minutes. A serious read requires checking appendices, benchmark release details, and any accompanying system card.84:T560,ReSkill decomposes repair into explicit components. Given a failed rollout, a frozen language model generates a set of failure hypotheses, a set of candidate local patches, and a coverage matrix indicating which hypotheses each patch addresses. The outer repair procedure maintains weights over active hypotheses, selects a patch by weighted coverage, applies it to the external skill set, and retests the edited skill set in the environment. If the retest still fails, the new failed trajectory is used to update the hypothesis set and their weights for the next round.

Two design choices matter. First, model weights remain frozen throughout; only the external skill set is edited. That makes repairs local, inspectable, and reversible. Second, repair state is maintained across rounds in structured form rather than compressed into a fresh natural-language reflection each time. This separates diagnosis, patch generation, patch selection, and feedback integration, reducing the noise that comes from asking the model to do all of them in one generation.

Relative to one-shot reflection methods such as Reflexion-style repair, ReSkill behaves more like an explicit credit-assignment loop over hypotheses and edits. The language model proposes structured repair factors, but the outer procedure decides which local edit to trust based on coverage and environment feedback.85:T444,The evaluation uses two interactive settings: ALFWorld for grounded household interaction and TextCraft from AgentGym for symbolic crafting. Together they form six benchmark–model combinations. Under fixed repair budgets, ReSkill improves final repair success by an average of 3.7 and 3.3 percentage points over two competitive repair baselines. The gains are moderate, but meaningful in a budgeted multi-round repair setting, where avoiding wasted edits matters.

From the analysis, the method helps most when failures have multiple plausible causes and when the first repair attempt reveals a new failure mode that should change which hypotheses are prioritized next. Because model weights are unchanged, the improvements are better interpreted as evidence for the repair procedure itself rather than for additional learned capability.

The limitations are also clear. The gains are not large, and the environments are still relatively controlled. Whether the same framework scales to more open-ended tool-use agents, coding agents, or real software environments remains an open question.86:T55d,SkillLift formulates skill evolution as a bilevel optimization problem. The upper level learns a rubric that matches oracle preferences over skills. The lower level optimizes a population of skills under that rubric. The central claim is that ranking is a smoother and cheaper target than absolute score prediction: it is enough to know which skill is better, not to estimate exact utility.

The optimization alternates between two phases. In the skill-update phase, the rubric is fixed and used as a cheap surrogate evaluator. It provides dense, structured feedback for revising candidate skills. In the rubric-update phase, the skill population is fixed and the oracle is called only to realign the rubric-induced ranking with the oracle ranking. This separates expensive evaluation from frequent search steps.

A practical point the paper emphasizes is oracle complexity. Many prior methods effectively pay O(LT) oracle cost, where L is the number of evolution rounds and T is the number of inner optimization epochs. SkillLift aims for O(L): the oracle is used for periodic realignment, while most search happens in the learned rubric space. The paper also positions the rubric as a structured evaluation space rather than a single scalar reward model, which matters because skill editing benefits from interpretable dimensions of feedback rather than one opaque score.87:T539,The paper starts with a pilot study on 10 WildClawBench tasks, 10 evolution rounds, and 3 rollout seeds per skill. The point of this study is diagnostic: direct skill optimization shows large best–worst gaps throughout training, indicating high variance and poor scaling, while oracle token cost dominates total compute and keeps growing.

The main experiments cover two complex agent benchmarks. The headline result in the paper is that SkillLift outperforms existing auto-skill evolution baselines even when those baselines are given a 2x larger token budget. It also reaches target performance with 40% to 70% lower token cost. The important takeaway is not only lower cost, but a better search regime: converting sparse oracle outcomes into an aligned rubric changes the efficiency frontier of skill improvement.

The most informative ablations are the conceptual ones: whether ranking targets are more stable than absolute-score targets, whether alternating optimization beats direct end-to-end skill revision, and whether dense rubric feedback reduces failure-patching behavior. Those are the experiments to inspect closely. If the full paper reports rubric–oracle rank correlation, sensitivity to oracle refresh frequency, or diversity of the skill population, those would be especially useful signals for judging scalability.88:T518,The central thesis is “substrate inversion” with two structural commitments:
- A single agent-native reasoning surface: the agent reads and reasons over one shared substrate expressed in a form the LLM can consume in one pass (Markdown is presented as a pragmatic instantiation, not a final primitive).
- Schema translation isolated at the action boundary: a Sync Agent (or boundary layer) compiles legacy typed data into the substrate and translates decisions back into executable actions, preventing repeated schema crossings during reasoning.
Two mechanisms justify why these commitments matter:
1) Context-bandwidth asymmetry: LLMs can ingest large, relationally rich prose in one forward pass, while field-by-field typed access strips relations at each schema boundary, forcing the model to reconstruct relational context in working memory.
2) Cross-loop coupling: action, skill, and policy improvement loops compound only if they share the same substrate; otherwise improvements remain siloed across incompatible representations.
Operationally, the paper sketches a four-layer framework (Data/Knowledge/Intelligence/Governance), introduces per-skill trust gradients, and discusses how safety risks (notably indirect prompt injection) are reshaped rather than eliminated by the substrate choice.3:["$","main",null,{"className":"flex-1 overflow-y-auto","children":["$","div",null,{"className":"mx-auto max-w-4xl px-4 py-8 sm:px-6 xl:max-w-6xl","children":["$","$L9",null,{"data":{"date":"2026-09-16","generated_at":"2026-09-16T01:45:36.110251+00:00","stats":{"total":147,"starred":3,"must_read":12,"skim":99,"glance":36,"by_category":{"pretraining_data":2,"llm_pretrain":5,"scaling":4,"architecture":9,"efficient":21,"eval":15,"multimodal":15,"multimodal_gen":8,"post_train":24,"mechanism":25,"coding_agent":4,"agent":15},"fetch_status":{}},"categories":[{"id":"pretraining_data","emoji":"📚","name":"预训练数据","description":"以预训练数据为核心贡献的工作：数据采集/清洗/去重、数据配比(data mixture)、数据筛选/质量评估、语料构建、数据课程(data curriculum)、数据重复(repetition)、合成数据用于预训练、Tokenizer/词表设计、成员推断/数据检测。判据是『论文核心是数据本身』，而非训练 recipe/优化器(→ llm_pretrain)或评测资产(→ eval)","papers":[{"id":"2609.15528","title":"To Each Language Its Tokenizer: Modular Tokenizers for Efficient Multilingual LLMs","abstract":"Multilingual Large Language Models (LLMs) traditionally rely on a single vocabulary shared by all supported languages, which can lead to uneven compression across them. Co-design:","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"must_read","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-11T17:18:04Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13356.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文要解决的是小参数量模型如何在数学推理和长程工具使用上接近大模型能力，同时把训练 attention，放在第 6、12、18、24、30 层，形成 5:1 的 local-to-global 比例。这个选择比全局注意力更省 KV cache 和带宽，同时通过周期性 global layer 保留跨长距离信息传播。模型还使用 QK normalization 和 partial RoPE，rotary fraction 为 256K。交互轨迹被改写为 MDP state-action transitions，使模型在多轮工具调用中获得更密集的 step-level 监督，而不是只对最终回答做监督。post-training 使用 execution-verified trajectories，并混合 think/no-think SFT：需要复杂推理时保留 deliberate Absorbed into data echoing in 2019, it has carried one objection -- that reuse merely imitates a larger learning rate -- and no baseline tuned as carefully as the method itself. This paper runs the missing test. 'Minibatch persistency' (reusing the same batch for K consecutive steps), proposed in 2018, aims to alleviate this but has faced a persistent critique: does reuse merely simulate a larger learning rate? Does it actually work when compared against a rigorously tuned baseline?","importance":"This is the most rigorous and comprehensive empirical validation of data reuse strategies (data echoing/minibatch persistency) to date. It not only addresses an eight-year-old methodological critique but also evaluates the costs and benefits across four dimensions: optimizer steps, fresh tokens, wall-clock time, and energy (joules). It provides solid empirical grounding for data-constrained scenarios (e.g., low-resource languages, domain-specific fine-tuning).","technical_approach":"The paper employs a pre-registered, rigorous experimental design, training a 49M parameter Transformer on FineWeb-Edu. The critical methodological choice is tuning the learning rate independently for every experimental cell (batch size B ∈ {32, 128, 512} and reuse factor K), alongside an A/A control arm to calibrate the noise floor. By measuring the resources required to reach a fixed validation loss, it isolates the effects on both optimization efficiency and system energy consumption.","experiments":"The results explicitly refute the critique that reuse is just a re-parameterized learning rate (the ratio of optimal learning rates at B=32 and 128 contradicts this hypothesis). It predicts conditional quantiles, so the output is probabilistic rather than a single point forecast. The corpus mixes curated real-world data from GIFT-Eval-Pretrain+ and BLAST with synthetic data from KernelSynth and CauKerV2. CauKerV2 extends causal-kernel generation to forecasting by sampling structural causal models and composing multiple temporal dynamics online during pretraining. The training recipe introduces a progressive convergence schedule, designed to yield locally converged intermediate checkpoints, and deep quantile supervision, which applies quantile losses to intermediate layers rather than only the final output. A parameter-efficient prompt-tuning module is then used for in-distribution forecasting adaptation while keeping the backbone frozen. The same pretrained backbone is reused for forecasting, classification, and anomaly detection.","experiments":"The released model supports contexts up to 8,192 observations. The reported result is that Tabby is competitive with recent time-series foundation models while using a relatively standard patch-Transformer backbone. This property makes MoE-based LLMs particularly attractive for resource-constrained distributed environments. However, federated fine-tuning of MoE-based LLMs remains challenging under heterogeneous client data. Since clients often correspond to different task preferences, directly aggregating the","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-11T18:04:41Z","category_id":"llm_pretrain","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13395.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"核心问题：在数据异质（不同客户端偏不同任务/领域）的联邦微调场景下，MoE LLM 的“稀疏专家”会因为简单聚合而丢失专家专门化（specialization），并在共享专家上出现梯度方向冲突（destructive interference），导致全局模型对各任务都变差。论文要解决的是：在不知道客户端真实任务偏好的情况下，如何做 task-aware 的联邦聚合，让 MoE 的路由结构继续保持分工。","importance":"与基座预训练的直接关系中等：它是 fine-tuning/FL 方向，不是预训练 recipe。但它对 MoE 训练动态与“专家专门化如何被更新破坏”给了一个可迁移的视角：路由统计可以作为任务/域的隐变量表征，用来约束更新的聚合方式。对做 MoE continual adaptation、跨域增量训练、或 post-train 反哺 pretrain（例如用路由信号做数据分桶/课程）的研究员，有一定启发。整体贡献更偏工程方法组合，创新点在于把 routing features→task coordinates→update reconstruction 这条链条系统化。","technical_approach":"方法叫 FedTAR（Task-awARe Federated fine-tuning）。关键做法是用 MoE 路由输出作为“任务表征”，再把它和参数更新联系起来，实现 task-aware 聚合：\n1）客户端本地微调时收集 routing statistics：平均专家利用率、专家共激活关系、路由置信度等，作为 routing feature。\n2）对 routing feature 做 SVD 得到低维 task coordinates（把不可观测的 task preference 映射到可聚类的坐标）。\n3）对本地更新（local updates）也做 SVD 提取主导更新方向（dominant update directions）。\n4）基于 task coordinates 做客户端聚类与“簇内/簇间”的 task-aware 聚合，避免把不同任务的更新无差别平均。\n5）学习 task space 与 update space 的映射，并用该映射重构（reconstruct）全局更新，使聚合后的更新仍对齐各任务的主方向，从而：a）保留专家专门化（不同任务主要更新不同专家），b）缓解共享专家上的方向冲突。\n与常见 FedAvg 或简单按样本数加权不同，FedTAR 的核心区别是：聚合权重与更新重构由路由行为驱动，而不是由显式任务标签或手工分组驱动。","experiments":"给出的全文片段只到方法动机与 分钟可理解方法主线；要评估可信度需要把实验与消融完整看完。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets federated fine-tuning of MoE-based LLMs under heterogeneous clients. Naïve aggregation (e.g., FedAvg) can (i) weaken expert specialization because task-specific update magnitudes get averaged away, and (ii) create destructive interference on shared experts when different client tasks push updates in conflicting directions. The challenge is to make aggregation task-aware without observing explicit task labels.","importance":"This is primarily a federated fine-tuning method rather than a pretraining recipe, so the direct impact on foundation-model pretraining is moderate. 只有在指标、可用信息和成本边界都与部署条件匹配时，才支持 allocation claim。作者总结出三个常见错配：一是把 coverage 当成 returned-answer accuracy，例如生成 100 个答案后只要有一个正确就算成功，但真实系统只能返回一个；二是实验用了部署时没有的信息，例如答案 key、离线难度排序或 oracle selector；三是成本口径不完整，只算生成 时，连贯性和全局一致性会明显变差。作者把这个问题和非自回归翻译里的 multi-modality problem 对齐，认为 DLM 的并行预测失真与 NAT 的独立位置预测是同一类病灶，只是 DLM 还多了一个迭代去噪、逐步锚定的约束。","importance":"这是对新型语言建模范式里一个关键问题的直接推进。DLM 近两年最常见的质疑之一，就是并行生成虽然快，但质量常被独立预测假设拖住，尤其在低步数推理时更明显。DA-DLM 的意义在于，它不是简单再调 schedule 或 confidence fixing，而是正面修改去噪时的条件独立假设，把“每步并行预测”改成“每步在一个结构化候选图上做协调选择”。如果这个方向成立，DLM 的质量-速度折中会更有希望，不必总靠增加 denoising steps 来补质量。对做 diffusion LM、masked/any-order LM、甚至 blockwise 并行生成的人，这篇文章都值得认真看，因为它提出的是建模层面的修正，而不是只做 inference trick。","technical_approach":"$15","experiments":"实验覆盖 language modeling、open-ended generation 和 summarization。主结论有两层。第一，DA-DLM 在这些设置上都稳定优于 BD3LM 基线。第二，收益在 denoising steps 更少时更大，这和方法动机是对齐的：步数越少，每步需要并行决定的 token 越多，独立预测带来的不一致越严重，因此显式依赖建模的边际价值更高。\n\n摘要和正文给出的定性结果里，一个很重要的点是：在 summarization 上，DA-DLM 可以做到与 autoregressive baseline 匹配，同时保留并行解码优势。这说明它的收益不只是开放生成里“看起来更顺”，而是在条件生成任务上也能把全局一致性拉回来。\n\n从全文结构推断，关键实验应该包括几类消融。其一，步数敏感性：比较不同 denoising step 数下 DA-DLM 与 BD3LM 的差距，验证少步场景收益更大。其二，DAG 设计消融：例如是否必须 position-oriented、anchor 是否必要、transition 是否只连相邻 group。其三，任务泛化：不仅看 perplexity 或 NLL，也看生成质量和下游 summarization。对这篇文章，最该盯的不是单个平均分，而是“随着步数减少，质量下降曲线是否明显变缓”。如果是，这说明它确实改善了 DLM 的核心结构问题，而不是只在高步数 regime 做小修补。\n\n给定内容里没有完整表格数字、参数规模和训练 token 数，但从论文叙述看，作者强调的是 consistent gains 和 low-step regime 的优势，而不是靠更大模型或更多数据取胜。阅读时应重点核对实验设置是否严格控制了推理预算、block size、fixing schedule structured denoising LM。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a structural weakness in diffusion language models. At each denoising step, standard DLMs predict many tokens in parallel but usually factorize them across positions, learning token-wise marginals rather than dependencies among the tokens generated in the same step. The result is a familiar failure mode: locally plausible tokens that do not form a coherent sequence together. The problem becomes more severe when the number of denoising steps is reduced, because each step must resolve more uncertainty in parallel. The authors connect this directly to the multi-modality problem in non-autoregressive translation, arguing that DLMs and NATs share the same independence pathology, with the extra complication that DLMs also rely on iterative anchoring across denoising steps.","importance":"This is a meaningful contribution to alternative language modeling paradigms. One of the main criticisms of DLMs is that parallel generation is attractive for speed, but quality is often limited by the conditional independence assumption, especially in low-step inference. DA-DLM matters because it does not merely tune schedules or confidence-based fixing. It changes the modeling assumption at the denoising step itself, replacing independent per-position prediction with coordinated selection over a structured candidate graph. If this line of work holds up, it improves the quality–speed trade-off of DLMs in a more fundamental way, reducing the need to recover quality simply by adding more denoising steps. Anyone working on diffusion LM, masked or any-order LM, or blockwise parallel generation should read it as a modeling paper, not just an inference tweak.","technical_approach":"$16","experiments":"$17","reading_guide":"$18","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"应立刻看方法和 ablation：它把 DLM 的独立预测问题改写成结构化依赖问题，可能改变对少步去噪质量上限的判断。","en":"Read the method and ablations now: it reframes DLM’s independent-token problem as structured dependency modeling and may shift priors on few-step denoising quality."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Tencent","Affiliation:55footnotemark:Tencent"],"authors":["Pengyu Ji","Zichen Zhang","Xiang Hu","Kewei Tu"],"notable_authors":["Kewei Tu"],"tags":["diffusion-LM","DAG","non-autoregressive","structured-generation","language-modeling"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15070","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.15070","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.15903","title":"Discrete Beckmann Transport Models for One-Step Language Modeling and Reasoning","abstract":"Discrete diffusion and flow models are a promising alternative to autoregressive language models, but compressing many-step sampling into fewer steps typically requires distilling a pretrained teacher model. s","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-13T03:37:48Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_14257.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是 diffusion language model 上语义水印难以落地的问题。现有语义水印大多依赖 AR 生成过程中的“先生成完整候选句子，再按密钥相关语义分数筛选接受”的范式；但 DLM 在去噪过程中会并行或任意顺序更新多个位置，局部 token 在中间步骤并不对应一个完整语义单元，因此传统句级候选-拒绝式语义水印无法直接套用。论文要回答的是：在不依赖完整句子候选的前提下，怎样把密钥相关的语义信号直接注入 DLM 的局部去噪更新，并在检测时抵抗改写、释义和边界漂移。","importance":"从基座预训练视角看，这篇论文的直接主题是 watermarking，不是预训练 recipe；但它对新型语言建模范式有明确相关性，因为它讨论的是 DLM 这种非 AR 生成机制下，哪些围绕生成过程构建的控制/约束技术需要被重新设计。很多围绕 ARLM 建立的方法——不只是水印，也包括控制生成、约束解码、验证器插入——都默认 prefix 完整、生成顺序固定。DenMark 说明，一旦换成扩散式离散生成，这些假设会失效，方法必须改成“对局部更新做前瞻估计”。这对研究 diffusion LM 的人有启发：DLM 的优势不只是并行生成和 infilling，它也要求一整套新的 decoding-time instrumentation。贡献量级上，它更像是 DLM 生态中的关键配套方法，而不是改变 DLM 训练范式本身的工作。","technical_approach":"作者提出 DenMark，核心思想是把语义水印从“对完整候选句子打分后再选”改成“对局部去噪更新做 rollout-guided semantic lookahead”。方法可以拆成三步：\n1. 固定区域划分。将待生成序列切成若干固定 token region，每个 region 作为语义证据注入和检测的基本单元。这样做不是因为 region 天然对应句法边界，而是为了在 DLM 的局部更新过程中定义一个稳定的操作粒度。\n2. 临时 rollout 估计语义后果。对于某个候选局部更新，当前 region 及其周围很多位置仍未完全确定，无法直接计算语义水印分数。DenMark 会对未决位置做临时补全 rollout，得到若干近似完成的 continuation，再用密钥相关的语义评分函数估计该局部更新最终会带来多大水印收益。随后选择估计分数最高的更新提交。这样，水印信号不是在句子完成后一次性注入，而是在整个去噪过程中逐步累积。\n3. 检测阶段的 calibrated scanning。语义攻击或改写会改变 token 边界和 region 对齐关系，如果仍按固定 region 切分检测，证据会被稀释。作者因此在多个候选 region size 上做扫描，并进行校准，以恢复边界漂移下的水印统计证据。\n论文的关键区别在于：它不把 DLM 强行改造成 AR 式候选生成流程，而是顺着 DLM 的迭代去噪机制，把语义评估前移到局部更新决策上。文中还给出分析，把最终语义分数提升解释为一系列局部选择贡献的累积，这为方法为何有效提供了比经验结果更清楚的机制说明。","experiments":"从提供的全文内容可以确认，实验覆盖多个 DLM 和多个数据集，并重点比较 DenMark 与 token-level watermark baseline 在语义攻击下的可检测性。作者声称 DenMark 在 semantic rewriting、paraphrasing 一类攻击后仍能保持更强的 watermark evidence，这与方法设计是一致的：它编码的是语义层面的密钥信号，而不是表层 token 偏置。\n当前片段没有给出完整实验表，因此不能可靠复述具体模型名称、检测 AUC、TPR@FPR、攻击强度分层结果或 rollout 次数带来的开销数字。能从正文确定的实验主线包括：\n1. 在多个 DLM 上验证方法通用性，而不是绑定单一扩散 LM；\n2. 与 token-level watermark 比较鲁棒性，尤其是在语义保持但表面形式变化的攻击下；\n3. 评估 calibrated scanning 对 region boundary shift 的修复作用；\n4. 分析 rollout-guided 局部选择如何累积成最终更高的语义水印分数。\n如果精读原文，建议重点找三类消融：region size 选择、rollout 数量/质量与检测性能的关系、以及不同攻击类型下 semantic watermark 与 token watermark 的失效模式差异。这些比单个总体指标更能说明方法边界。","reading_guide":"这篇适合带着问题泛读。先看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries，确认作者指出的那个关键错位：AR 语义水印依赖完整候选，DLM 中间状态没有完整语义单元。然后重点看方法部分里 region partition、temporary rollout、以及 detection 时的 calibrated scanning，这三块构成了 DenMark 的主体。实验部分优先看鲁棒性比较和消融，不必过度关注常规生成质量指标，除非论文专门报告了水印强度与文本质量的权衡。阅读时建议一直问两个问题：第一，rollout 的语义估计是否足够稳定，还是会引入很大方差；第二，固定 region 设计在长文本和自由改写下是否会成为瓶颈。预计 25–35 分钟可以完成一次有效泛读；若你在做 diffusion LM decoding/control，再花时间细看检测统计和复杂度分析。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a mismatch between semantic watermarking methods and diffusion language models. Existing semantic watermarks are mostly built for autoregressive generation, where one can complete a sentence or span, score it with a key-dependent semantic criterion, reject bad candidates, and then continue. That workflow breaks in DLMs because denoising updates multiple positions iteratively, often before any semantic unit is complete. The problem is therefore how to inject a semantic watermark directly into local denoising decisions, without relying on fully formed sentence candidates, while remaining robust to paraphrasing, rewriting, and boundary shifts at detection time.","importance":"For pretraining researchers, the paper is adjacent rather than central: it is about watermarking, not training recipes. Still, it is relevant to alternative LM paradigms because it shows that methods built around the generation process do not transfer automatically from ARLMs to DLMs. Many AR-era techniques assume a fixed left-to-right prefix and completed candidate units. DenMark shows that once generation becomes iterative and any-order, those assumptions fail, and control mechanisms must move toward local-update lookahead. That is a useful lesson for diffusion LM research more broadly. The contribution is best viewed as an enabling method for the DLM ecosystem rather than a change to the core DLM training paradigm.","technical_approach":"$1f","experiments":"$20","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper, but it is worth reading with a specific question in mind. Start with the introduction and preliminaries to understand the core mismatch: AR semantic watermarking assumes completed candidates, while DLM intermediate states do not provide them. Then focus on the method sections covering region partitioning, temporary rollouts, and calibrated scanning. Those three pieces are the substance of DenMark. In the experiments, prioritize robustness comparisons and ablations rather than generic generation-quality numbers, unless the paper explicitly studies the quality–watermark tradeoff. Two questions are especially useful while reading: whether rollout-based semantic estimation is stable enough in practice, and whether fixed-region design becomes a bottleneck for long-form or heavily rewritten text. The key contribution is to make token-indexed memory context-aware while keeping the lookup pattern simple. A learned gate, conditioned on the current hidden state, selects or mixes among those slots at each position. The design preserves the efficiency of token-indexed access: the token first narrows the candidate memory set, and the context then chooses the appropriate component. Compared with Per-Layer Embedding, Value Embedding, STEM, Engram, and Bigram-style methods, the main change is not only where memory is injected, but how it is addressed. Prior methods may use context-dependent fusion, but the retrieved memory vector is still usually determined by the token or n-gram. These methods are widely applicable for designing efficient networks in society, from river basins and blood vessels to mail and gas distribution systems. However, they remain understudied in the context of designing deep generative models, particularly at a large scale. Standard continuous-time generative models, such as the flow mat","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T05:42:31Z","category_id":"architecture","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15072.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的问题，是把 branched optimal transport（BOT，分支最优传输）从小规模、难优化的几何问题，改造成可在现代生成模型里训练的连续、可摊销、可扩展建模框架。现有 flow matching / rectified flow 一类连续生成方法，默认每个样本沿各自路径独立运输，偏向直线或局部平滑的位移；这对层级结构强、模态之间共享前缀明显的数据并不合适。论文抓住的关键点是：很多真实数据的生成过程更像“先共享主干，再局部分叉”，而不是所有样本从噪声端各走各的。难点在于 BOT 的经典求解通常依赖离散网络优化、组合搜索或 PDE，计算代价高，而且难以嵌入大模型训练。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接影响不在于它立刻能替代主流 LLM 训练目标，而在于它提出了一种不同于 next-token AR、也不同于标准 diffusion / flow 的生成归纳偏置：显式鼓励样本共享生成路径。这个偏置对层级语义、类簇结构、多模态族群、以及“先粗后细”的生成过程是自然的。若把这个思路迁移到离散 token、语义 latent、视觉 tokenizer 或多模态统一表示，潜在价值在于两点：一是更好地建模共享中间表示，二是把生成过程中的“公共前缀计算”从经验现象变成目标函数的一部分。贡献类型更偏建模范式与优化框架，而不是现成的预训练 recipe。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 BOTA（Branched Optimal Transport Amortization）。从全文表述看，它不是直接求解离散 BOT 树，而是把 BOT 的“规模经济”原则转写成一个连续优化目标，再用神经网络参数化的流场去摊销求解。论文延续了 conditional flow matching 的训练范式，但目标不再是给定 source-target 对后学习独立插值速度，而是学习一种允许不同样本在中间时刻汇合、共享轨迹、之后再分叉的运输机制。\n\n关键区别有三层。第一，标准 CFM/FM 的条件路径通常由单对样本决定，样本之间没有耦合；BOTA 则把耦合放进目标里，让多条路径共享一段运输更便宜。第二，经典 BOT 常在图结构上定义边成本，成本对流量是次可加的，典型形式与 m^alpha（0<alpha<1）相关；BOTA 的创新在于把这种“合流更省”的偏好转成可微、可训练的连续形式，使其能和神经 ODE / flow matching 兼容。第三，它强调 amortization：不是每次对一个数据集实例重新解 BOT，而是训练一个网络，在不同 batch、不同样本上复用学到的 branched transport 规律。\n\n从引言和背景部分能看出，论文技术路线大致包括：先定义连续密度路径与速度场；再构造一个带 BOT 诱导正则或能量项的优化问题，使最优流场倾向于形成树状共享主干；最后用 flow-matching 风格的监督或自监督目标训练神经向量场。这里最值得注意的不是“又一个 flow matching 变体”，而是它试图把样本间协同运输纳入训练目标。对生成建模来说，这相当于把独立粒子视角改成网络流视角。","experiments":"从全文可见，实验重点放在两类任务：生物数据生成和图像生成。作者想证明的不是单一指标上的小幅提升，而是模型确实学到了有层级含义的生成路径。摘要和引言已经明确声称，BOTA 能在具有内在层级结构的数据上恢复“meaningful hierarchical generative processes”。这类结论通常会通过三种证据支撑：\n\n1. 可视化证据：中间时刻样本轨迹先聚合成共享干线，再在后期分叉到不同簇或类别，而标准 FM 更像彼此独立的平滑位移。\n2. 生成质量或似然相关指标：在图像或生物分布建模上达到与标准连续生成模型可比或更好的结果。\n3. 结构性消融：改变 BOT 成本中的分支强度参数（如 alpha）或去掉 branched regularization 后，树状结构减弱，性能或可解释性下降。\n\n当前给出的全文片段没有完整表格和具体数字，因此无法可靠填写参数量、数据规模、FID/NLL 等精确数值。可以确认的是，作者把“可扩展性”作为主张的一部分，说明实验不只停留在二维 toy data，而是上到了更复杂的生物与图像任务。精读时应重点核对：是否真的在较高维数据上稳定训练； branched 结构是否来自目标函数，而不是可视化选择偏差；以及在相同 backbone、相同训练预算下，相比 FM/Rectified Flow 的收益到底是生成质量、样本效率，还是仅仅更好看。","reading_guide":"泛读建议先抓三处。第一，看 Introduction 里对 BOT 与标准 OT / FM continuous-time generative models: they move samples largely as independent particles, which is a poor inductive bias for data with hierarchical or tree-like organization. Branched optimal transport (BOT) offers the right geometry because it rewards shared transport before branching, but classical BOT is computationally hard, often combinatorial, and not suitable for large-scale neural training. 分布；这篇工作说明，统一模型并不意味着统一推理路径。对预训练研究员有两点启发。第一，层间表征的可读出性和任务/域相关性很强，说明中间层已经包含相当多可直接解码的信息，而这些信息的成熟深度依赖流量类型，这对训练中如何塑造层级语义、是否要显式鼓励中间层可用性，有参考价值。第二，部署流量条件化的计算分配，与训练阶段的数据配比、课程学习、domain specialization It studies a deployment question: given that each application sees a narrow and predictable prompt distribution, how much inference depth can be removed, and how should early-exit policies be configured for that specific traffic? The target setting is single-user, edge-style inference, where every generated token normally pays full-layer cost even though many tokens may already be determined at intermediate layers. Two practical obstacles frame the problem: exit decisions must be cheap enough to preserve savings, and early-exited tokens leave missing upper-layer KV states for future attention. The central question is therefore how to design and evaluate traffic-conditioned early-exit procedures for frozen LMs, and how much extra efficiency comes from knowing the deployment’s actual query distribution in advance.","importance":"Its value for foundation-model researchers is indirect but real. The paper makes a concrete case that a single pretrained model should not necessarily use a single inference depth across deployments. That matters because pretraining optimizes broad capability, while real traffic is highly skewed. The results suggest that intermediate-layer decodability is strongly domain-dependent: some traffic types become predictable much earlier than others. This has implications for how we think about layerwise representation formation during pretraining, whether intermediate states should be made more directly usable, and how domain specialization appears inside a general model. It also connects deployment-time compute allocation with training-time questions such as data mixture, curriculum, and specialization. This is not a paradigm-shifting paper, but it pushes early exit from generic benchmark acceleration toward traffic-aware deployment configuration, which is a meaningful systems contribution.","technical_approach":"$23","experiments":"$24","reading_guide":"This is a skim paper. Start with the introduction, because it defines the deployment setting carefully and explains why the goal is not to train specialized models but to run the same pretrained model at different effective depths. Then read the method sections on the readout, confidence test, and the layer-equivalent cost metric. Those parts explain why the authors insist on a frozen backbone and self-distillation-style supervision. In the experiments, focus on the quality–cost curves across traffic types and the comparison between traffic-aware and generic configurations. If time is limited, keep two questions in mind: whether most of the gain comes from traffic specialization, and whether missing upper-layer KV states are a practical blocker. traject","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T12:55:08Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15504.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作检查的是 分数接近、平均 acceptance 高、输出质量相近，视作与原模型等价；这篇文章指出这三者都不能替代 trajectory equivalence。这个提醒对预训练研究也有价值，因为越来越多 pretrain 范式会在推理时引入并行预测、辅助头、diffusion view Forward。这对所有依赖“冻结主干 + 辅助并行头”的方法都有参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法上，这篇文章做了两件事。第一，独立复现 Orthrus 的训练与推理，并搭建了可控框架，系统改变训练数据分布、目标和超参，避免结论只依赖原作者 checkpoint。第二，围绕数值精度做 sequence-level equivalence 检验，而不是只看任务分数。\n\n在训练部分，作者冻结 AR backbone，训练一个 diffusion view 来并行预测未来多个 token。关键改动是蒸馏数据来源：不是直接拿通用语料 continuation，而是从公开数据集中只取 prompt，再让冻结的 Qwen3-1.7B 用 greedy decoding 生成 response，形成 teacher-generated distillation corpus。这样训练样本更接近模型自己推理时会访问到的前缀状态，减少 teacher forcing 与 on-policy generation 的分布偏移。数据过滤保留 50 到 1000 字符的 prompt，最终得到 4,113,358 个 prompt-response 样本，训练在 8 张 H100 上完成。\n\n在评测部分，作者把“无损”定义为与对应 frozen AR 模型逐 token 完全一致，而不是语义相近或分数相近。然后分别在不同数值精度下运行 Orthrus 与 AR baseline，直接比较生成轨迹是否完全相同。论文的核心论点是：Orthrus 的共识机制在理想算术模型下可以保持等价，但有限精度实现会让 logits、缓存状态或归一化过程出现微小偏差；由于解码是离散 argmax/采样过程，这些微小偏差会在某一步跨过决策边界，最终导致整个后续轨迹分叉。作者还进一步分析了分叉概率与 reference model 的 response-conditional perplexity 的关系，发现当参考模型对答案更不确定时，微小数值扰动更容易改变最终 token 选择。","experiments":"关键结果有三组。\n\n第一组是最核心的 losslessness 检验。作者在 12 个领域、共 1,190 个 prompts 上比较 Orthrus 与对应 AR 模型的精确轨迹匹配率。对作者发布的 checkpoint，在 BF16 推理下 exact trajectory matching 只有 45%；对他们独立训练的同容量模型，这个数字是 43%。也就是说，超过一半样本会在某一步出现 token 级分叉。相同评测在 FP32 下则可以得到精确轨迹匹配，说明问题不是算法逻辑错误，而是有限精度实现触发了离散决策差异。\n\n第二组是数据分布对并行效率的影响。作者用 teacher-generated、on-policy 蒸馏数据训练出的独立模型，在多数评测域上 Tokens Per Forward 高于原始 released checkpoint。摘要里没有给出完整逐域数字，但全文强调这一提升是稳定出现的，说明 diffusion view 的训练分布如果更贴近 backbone 自身生成轨迹，就更容易学到可被共识机制接受的并行 proposal。\n\n第三组是“分叉不等于任务退化”。论文指出，尽管 BF16 下序列级不再严格一致，标准 benchmark 分数并不一定下降，某些实验里 Orthrus 的任务分数甚至略高于对应 AR 模型。这一结果很重要，因为它说明 benchmark equality 不能证明 exact equivalence，反过来，轻微数值扰动也可能偶然把答案推向 benchmark 更偏好的表述。作者还观察到 exact matching 概率与 response-conditional perplexity 强相关：参考模型越不确定，越容易被数值误差推离原轨迹。","reading_guide":"泛读时建议先看 Introduction，把作者想纠正的对象抓准：不是否定 Orthrus 的加速价值，而是限定“lossless”这个词的适用条件。然后直接看实验部分里关于 BF16 与 FP32 的 trajectory matching 对比，这是全文最有信息量的结果。接着看训练设置，重点关注 teacher-generated distillation corpus 的构造方式，以及作者为什么认为通用 human-written continuation 不足以训练 diffusion view。最后看与 perplexity 相关的分析，这部分能帮助理解为什么有些样本对数值误差更敏感。\n\n如果阅读时间有限，优先顺序可以是：Introduction → 轨迹一致性主实验图表 → 训练数据构造 → 误差与 perplexity 关系。预计 20 到 30 分钟可以抓住主要结论。值得带着问题去看的是：你所在系统里所谓“无损”究竟指 token-level identical、distributionally equivalent，还是 benchmark-preserving；这篇文章给了一个很实用的检查框架。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper tests whether Orthrus’s claim of “lossless speculative decoding” survives real numerical implementations. The key issue is not the ideal algorithmic argument, but operational equivalence under finite-precision arithmetic, stateful KV-cache updates, and discrete token selection. The conclusion is precise: exact trajectory equivalence can be observed under FP32, but under BF16 the accelerated system diverges from the frozen AR backbone on a substantial fraction of prompts. So “lossless” needs an explicit definition tied to precision and implementation details.","importance":"Its value is less about proposing a new decoding method and more about tightening the standard of evidence for inference-preserving acceleration. In practice, many papers treat similar benchmark scores, high acceptance rates, or near-identical outputs as sufficient evidence of equivalence to the base model. This work shows that none of these is a substitute for sequence-level trajectory identity. That matters for speculative decoding, hybrid AR+diffusion systems, and any setup that adds auxiliary parallel heads on top of a frozen LM. A second useful contribution is on the data side: training the diffusion view on teacher-generated, on-policy continuations improves Tokens Per Forward relative to generic teacher-forced human continuations. rep","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-08-16T03:41:37Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13205.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.IR","cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长上下文推理里，prefill QJL/TurboQuant 的变换域压缩经验）。在该域里：\n- 取 transformed key 的逐坐标符号位（sign）作为 1-bit token-level 索引。相似的 query/key 在符号上更一致，符号一致度可作为角相似度的粗估计。\n- 因为 sign 位本来就属于 key 的压缩表示的一部分，所以检索不需要额外索引结构；同时与低比特 KV 压缩（如 TurboQuant 这类）在 payload 预算上可对齐。\n\n为覆盖 prefill 与 decode：\n- Prefill：做 query grouping，把一组 query 共享一次候选 token 选择以摊薄开销。为避免平均池化导致符号抵消，提出 absmax-sign pooling：对每个坐标取绝对值最大的 query 的符号，尽量保留“组内任一 query 重要证据”。\n- Decode：用 packed 1-bit sign agreement 做快速打分/筛选，再用 norm-weighted agreement 补回仅用符号丢失的尺度信息。\n- 工程实现：提供 GQA-aware kernel，利用位运算加速 1-bit 匹配，并与稀疏注意力算子衔接。","experiments":"论文报告的关键结果（以文中给出的数字为准）：\n- 在 Qwen3.6 与 Every deployed system fixes that order by a single global convention. We prove this is optimal only when requests contain at most two pieces, and asymptotically wrong in general. Our main result is a structure theorem: the minimum prefix-trie co","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-12T04:02:31Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13692.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.DS","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"LLM 推理服务中的前缀缓存（Prefix Caching）复用率优化问题。当前系统（如 vLLM）均采用单一全局顺序（如按检索排名）拼接 prompt 中的可复用块，论文证明这种做法在块数大于 2 时并非最优，会导致缓存命中率下降和计算浪费。","importance":"从理论层面揭示了当前 LLM Serving 系统中前缀缓存策略的根本缺陷，将 prompt 块排序问题转化为层次聚类问题，为多块拼接场景（如 RAG、Agent、多轮对话）的推理优化提供了新的理论框架和近似算法。","technical_approach":"论文提出一个结构定理：最小前缀 Trie 成本等价于在请求的二叉层次结构上求最小化成本。这表明选择块顺序等同于选择一种聚类方式，从而消除了排列组合的复杂性。基于此，论文证明了全局顺序在最坏情况下会导致 $\\Theta(n^2)$ 的成本，而最优解为 $\\Theta(n\\log n)$。针对实际应用，提出了一种基于公共交集的凝聚聚类近似算法。","experiments":"在三个 BEIR 语料库的生产级 RAG 追踪数据上，该方法相比生产环境基线减少了 17-36% 的 prefill 计算量，且随着检索深度的增加，差距如理论预测般扩大。在保持生成质量约束下，仍能保留 13-23% 的收益。","reading_guide":"建议重点阅读 Section 3 的结构定理（将排列问题转化为聚类问题是核心洞察）和 Section 6 的近似算法。预计阅读时间 20 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The optimization of prefix caching reuse in LLM serving. Current systems (e.g., vLLM) use a single global order to assemble reusable chunks in prompts, which the paper proves is suboptimal when the number of chunks exceeds two, leading to decreased cache hit rates and wasted computation.","importance":"Theoretically exposes a fundamental flaw in the prefix caching strategies of current LLM serving systems. It reformulates prompt chunk ordering as a hierarchical clustering problem, providing a new theoretical framework and approximation algorithms for inference optimization in multi-chunk scenarios (e.g., RAG, Agents, multi-turn dialogue).","technical_approach":"The paper introduces a structure theorem: the minimum prefix-trie cost is equivalent to minimizing the cost over binary hierarchies on the requests. This shows that choosing chunk orders is equivalent to choosing a clustering, eliminating the complexity of permutations. Based on this, it proves that a global order incurs a $\\Theta(n^2)$ cost in the worst case, while the optimal is $\\Theta(n\\log n)$. For practical application, it proposes an agglomerative clustering approximation algorithm based on common intersections.","experiments":"On production RAG traces from three BEIR corpora, the method reduces prefill computation by 17-36% compared to production baselines, with the gap widening with retrieval depth as predicted by theory. Every device adds its own partial output to its residual stream (and broadcasts it) immediately, but only gathers (receives) the other devices' partials $δ$ modules later. A TP to DTP change therefore amounts to a real architecture change, and dense Transformer models need to be retrained or distilled after adaptation. We show that DTP breaks the permutation symmetry of neurons inside FFNs ","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-12T10:01:32Z","category_id":"efficient","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13846.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.CL","cs.DC"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文解决的是 Delayed Tensor Parallelism 中一个容易被忽略的问题：DTP 不是普通 tensor parallel 的通信实现替换，而是改变了网络函数。由于每个设备在若干模块后才看到其他设备的 partial output，FFN neuron 和 attention KV head 的分片位置会直接影响信息是否能在相邻模块中被及时使用。原本在 dense Transformer 里近似等价的 neuron/head permutation，在 DTP 下变成了有质量差异的架构选择。","importance":"对预训练研究的启发主要在训练系统和架构接口。DTP 这类低延迟推理结构需要 retrain 或 distill 才能恢复质量，因此分片布局会影响适配数据效率和训练收敛速度。论文把 sharding 从工程实现细节提升为可优化的结构参数，并给出一个低成本校准方法。它不改变基础 LM 目标，但对服务友好架构的预训练后适配、蒸馏成本控制、以及通信延迟约束下的模型设计有直接参考价值。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 affinity-aware sharding。作者利用 dense 模型中 FFN neuron 和 attention KV group 的 permutation symmetry：在不改变 dense 模型输出的前提下，可以重新排列 neuron 和 KV group，再映射到不同设备。DTP 会破坏这种对称性，因此需要选择更合适的排列。论文用校准数据估计两类 affinity：一类是前一层 KV group 对后一层 FFN neuron 的影响，另一类是 FFN neuron 对下一层 KV group 的影响。这个 affinity 被解释为一阶损伤估计，即某个 neuron 缺失某个 head contribution 时对 residual stream 造成的影响。优化上，给定相邻 head partition 后，对每层 neuron 做精确的 balanced linear assignment；给定 neuron assignment 后，对 8 个 KV group 分到 4 个设备的 2520 种 balanced labelled partition 做穷举搜索。两者交替直到收敛。校准和优化总耗时低于单张 GPU 两分钟。","experiments":"实验在 Qwen3-0.6B 和 Danube3-500M 上做 4 设备、δ=1 的 DTP 适配，并用相同 recipe 从 dense teacher 蒸馏。结果显示，优化布局在 Qwen3-0.6B 上所有 seed 都优于 contiguous 和 random layout；在 Danube3-500M 上 8 个 seed 中有 7 个优于对照。优化 layout 达到 contiguous layout 训练 1000 step 的质量，只需要 Qwen3-0.6B 的 347 step 和 Danube3-500M 的 565 step。训练到 10k step 时，优化 layout 达到相同 KL 的步数约为 contiguous 的一半到三分之二；在 Qwen3-0.6B 上没有看到差距自然闭合。消融结果也有信息量：co-located affinity 能预测 17 个 layout 的训练后 KL；主要收益来自 neuron assignment，head assignment 没有可测的额外收益；zero-shot damage 不能可靠预测训练后的质量。","reading_guide":"泛读建议 20 到 30 分钟。先看 Introduction 里对 DTP δ=1 的直观解释，确认它为什么是架构变化而不是通信实现变化。再看 §3 的 affinity 定义和一阶损伤估计，这是主要方法。优化算法可以重点看 balanced assignment 和 KV partition 交替优化的部分，不必深究实现细节。实验部分优先看 §5 中 layout 对 KL 收敛速度的影响、17 个 layout 的相关性分析，以及 neuron/head assignment 的消融。最值得深挖的是一个负结果：zero-shot DTP damage 不能预测蒸馏后的质量，这说明适配动态会改变布局评价标准。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a concrete failure mode in Delayed Tensor Parallelism: DTP is not just a communication optimization for standard tensor parallelism. By letting each device proceed with a residual stream that temporarily misses other devices' recent partial outputs, DTP changes the function computed by the network. As a result, the placement of FFN neurons and attention KV groups across devices becomes a quality-critical architectural choice.","importance":"The main relevance is at the boundary between efficient inference architecture and model adaptation. DTP can reduce decode latency by removing blocking all-reduces, but dense checkpoints need retraining or distillation after conversion. This work shows that the sharding layout can materially change adaptation efficiency. For pretraining researchers, the useful lesson is that communication-aware architectures may introduce hidden symmetry breaking, and those choices can affect the amount of distillation needed to recover the dense model.","technical_approach":"The method exploits the permutation symmetry of dense Transformers. In a standard dense model, FFN neurons and attention heads or KV groups can be permuted without changing the model output up to numerical noise. Under DTP, those permutations are no longer neutral because a local FFN only has immediate access to the preceding attention output produced on the same device. The paper defines calibration-based affinity scores between KV groups and FFN neurons, and between FFN neurons and the next layer's KV groups. These scores approximate the first-order damage caused when a neuron loses access to a head contribution. The resulting layout problem is solved by alternating two steps: an exact balanced linear assignment for neurons given neighboring head partitions, and an exhaustive search over the 2520 balanced labelled partitions of 8 KV groups over 4 devices given neuron assignments. The calibration and optimization pass takes under two minutes on one GPU.","experiments":"The evaluation converts two pretrained models, Qwen3-0.6B and Danube3-500M, to 4-device DTP with delay δ=1 and distills them from the dense model using the same training recipe across layouts. On Qwen3-0.6B, every optimized seed beats every contiguous and random layout. On Danube3-500M, seven out of eight optimized seeds beat the baselines. The optimized layout reaches the quality of the contiguous 1000-step run in 347 steps on Qwen3-0.6B and 565 steps on Danube3-500M. Over 10k steps, the optimized layout reaches the same KL values in roughly one half to two thirds of the steps required by the contiguous layout, with no clear gap closure on Qwen3-0.6B. The ablations are important: co-located affinity predicts trained KL across 17 layouts; most of the gain comes from neuron assignment; head assignment adds no measurable benefit; zero-shot layout damage is not predictive of post-distillation quality.","reading_guide":"A 20-30 minute skim is enough. Start with the introduction and the δ=1 DTP formulation to understand why DTP changes the model function. Then read the affinity definition and the alternating optimization procedure in the method section. In the experiments, focus on the KL convergence curves, the 17-layout correlation analysis, and the neuron-versus-head assignment ablation. The most useful detail is the mismatch between zero-shot damage and trained quality, because it shows that adaptation dynamics matter more than the immediate functional perturbation.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"关注 Delayed Tensor Parallelism (DTP) 对模型架构对称性的破坏，以及通过 affinity-aware sharding 减少通信延迟/加速重训练的系统级优化思路。","en":"Pay attention to how Delayed Tensor Parallelism (DTP) breaks model architecture symmetry, and the system-level optimization idea of using affinity-aware sharding to reduce communication latency and accelerate retraining."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Eloi de Reynal"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Tensor Parallelism","Delayed Tensor Parallelism","Model Sharding","Inference Optimization"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13846","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.13846","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.14850","title":"Self-Orchestrating Language Models: Leveraging Semantic Dependence for Efficient Inference","abstract":"Large language models (LLMs) demonstrate impressive capabilities, but their deployment presents significant efficiency challenges. Discrete diffusion models can generate in parallel but struggle to match autoregressive quality without many diffusion denoising steps. Long-context reasoning creates memory bottlenecks that strain even state-of-the-art accelerators. The proposed direction is to let the model annotate those dependencies and let the runtime use them to parallelize generation, evict intermediate context, or choose generation orders.","importance":"Its relevance to foundation-model research is mainly at the modeling-system interface. The model is trained not only to emit text, but also to emit executable control annotations. This creates a path between high-quality AR decoding and more parallel non-AR generation: keep the AR model where needed, but avoid treating every previous token as a hard dependency. It also suggests that training objectives may need to include structured execution metadata, not just next-token likelihood or instruction-following targets.","technical_approach":"The proposed family is called self-orchestrating language models. Each system has three components: an annotation language for semantic dependence, a runtime that interprets the annotations, and a training procedure that teaches the model to produce useful annotations. PASTA segments a response into chunks, where each chunk corresponds to one semantic unit. Special tokens mark independent chunks and synchronization barriers. The runtime launches asynchronous decoding threads for independent chunks and synchronizes them when dependencies appear. Training uses supervised finetuning on LLM-annotated data, followed by preference optimization that trades off response quality and latency. The work also describes Thinking in Place, where long reasoning traces are treated as bounded working memory and the model learns which intermediate steps to evict when memory is full, rather than relying on fixed-window or attention-score heuristics.","experiments":"For PASTA, the reported geometric-mean speedup is 1.21× to 1.93× on AlpacaEval, with quality changes ranging from +2.2% to -7.1%. The claimed advantage is a better quality-latency frontier than asynchronous decoding baselines such as Skeleton-of-Thought and APAR. The experimental message is not just raw speed; it is that annotation granularity, synchronization policy, and preference optimization jointly determine the quality-efficiency frontier. The TIP discussion targets long reasoning traces with tens of thousands of tokens, where KV-cache memory becomes the bottleneck and hand-crafted policies such as StreamingLLM, H2O, and SpAtten may fail to preserve semantically important state.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20–30 minutes. KV-cache pressure because trajectories are long and repeatedly alternate among phases such as thinking, acting, tool observation, and other context processing. Existing KV-eviction methods often score cached keys using recent queries, but in agentic workloads recent queries may only represent the current phase and may undervalue tokens needed by a later phase.","importance":"The contribution is mainly on the inference and serving side, but it is relevant to long-context model research. The key observation is that an agent trajectory should not be treated as a stationary query stream. Phase changes induce different query distributions. This has implications for training memory-aware models: phase structure, context compression, and retention policies may need to be represented explicitly rather than inferred from recency alone.","technical_approach":"AgentKV frames KV eviction as ranking cached keys by expected attention under an estimated future-query distribution. Recency-based methods such as SnapKV-style policies and R-KV estimate this distribution from the most recent query window. The paper argues that this estimator is biased for agentic trajectories. Principal-angle analysis shows that queries from think, act, tool, and others phases occupy distinguishable subspaces. AgentKV therefore maintains a small query buffer for each phase and scores candidate keys against the union of phase-specific representatives.
