spa","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-12T03:13:54Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13680.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作讨论的不是“怎样把目标任务分数做高”，而是“在给定行为漂移预算的前提下，怎样选到更好的微调方向”。作者把 instruct 模型微调中的行为退化——尤其是推理能力、通用能力和已有对齐行为的损失——从训练后的副作用，改写成训练前就应显式约束的量。论文关心的核心场景很尖锐：起点是已经具备显式推理能力的强 instruct 模型，但微调数据只给 question-answer，不给 reasoning trace。此时标准 SFT 往往能把目标任务做上去，却会持续侵蚀模型原有的推理行为。作者要解决的是：这种失败到底是监督信号不够导致的必然结果，还是优化走错了方向；如果只允许有限漂移，是否存在能兼顾目标任务与原有能力的更新方向。","importance":"对预训练/后训练接口的启发主要有三层。第一，它把 SFT 的分析坐标从参数空间或正则项，换成了“相对参考模型的行为漂移”，这比单纯看 loss 或 KL 更接近实际模型维护问题。第二，它提出一个很实用的判断：在固定漂移下，不同微调方法的差别主要不是“走多远”，而是“能走哪些方向”。这对理解 full FT、LoRA、参数选择式微调的能力边界很有帮助。第三，论文给出一个偏反直觉的结果：即便没有 CoT 监督，只要方向选得对，QA-only 微调也未必会破坏推理；反过来，方向不对时，更多自由度反而更容易把已有能力换掉。这个结论对后训练 recipe、continual adaptation、甚至 RL 初始化都很有参考价值，因为它提示很多所谓“数据不够好”的问题，部分其实是更新子空间设计不对。","technical_approach":"方法上有两个关键部分。第一部分是局部几何建模。作者围绕参考模型建立一个共享的行为坐标系：参考模型是原点，行为漂移预算定义局部边界；在这个局部区域里，漂移大小决定与原模型的距离，而更新方向决定预算被分配到哪些行为变化上。这样一来，full fine-tuning、LoRA、layer-selective tuning 虽然参数化方式不同，但都可以在“同等行为漂移”下比较其方向效率，即单位漂移带来的目标任务收益。\n\n第二部分是一个很朴素但有效的 layer-selective probe。作者不先设计复杂正则，而是系统扫描不同层组合的可训练子集，在相同数据、相同目标、相近漂移预算下比较哪些层的更新方向更高效、更稳定。论文强调这不是为了做最细粒度的架构搜索，而是把“可访问方向”作为实验变量隔离出来。结果显示，粗粒度的层选择就足以找到多个“近邻好方向”：它们能提升 QA-only 目标任务，同时保住推理和通用能力。相比之下，full FT 的方向空间太大，容易沿着牺牲原有行为的方向前进；LoRA 虽然也限制方向，但其低秩子空间未必总能覆盖这些高效方向，因此有时有效、有时无效。\n\n从机制解释上，作者的论点是：QA-only 监督没有直接指定“如何推理”，因此优化器会优先利用最容易降低训练损失的行为改写路径。若方向空间允许模型通过压缩、绕开或替换原有 reasoning style 来拟合答案，标准 SFT 就会这么做。层选择相当于人为改变可达更新子空间，让模型更倾向于在保留原有推理回路的前提下完成适配。这和常见的“加正则抑制漂移”不同：这里不是在同一方向上减小步长，而是换一组方向。","experiments":"实验覆盖 Qwen3-8B 和 Qwen3-14B 两个基座，任务包括科学推理和 100+ 语言翻译。最关键的现象实验是：对已经依赖显式 reasoning trajectory 的 instruct 模型做 QA-only 微调时，直接 full FT 会随着功能漂移增加而持续损害下游推理表现；也就是说，训练集目标分数上升和推理能力下降可以同时发生。LoRA 在部分设置下能缓解，但不稳定。layer-selective probe 则能找到多组更新层配置，在相近漂移水平下同时实现三件事：目标任务提升、推理能力保持、通用能力保持。\n\n翻译实验是论文里最有分量的应用验证。作者声称所得模型在 100 多个语言方向上相对参考模型有稳定提升，并且可与专门的多语翻译系统 Seed-X-PPO-7B、Tower-Plus-9B 对比，达到匹配或更好的结果。更重要的是，它不仅优于同尺寸 Qwen3-8B 的 RL 版翻译模型，而且把同一套 RL pipeline 接在这个初始化上还能继续得到更大增益，最终性能最好。这个结果说明方向选择带来的收益不是一次性的 SFT 偶然现象，而是会改变后续 RL 的可优化起点。\n\n从消融角度，值得注意的不是某一个绝对数字，而是控制变量做得比较干净：数据、目标函数、优化过程尽量固定，只改可训练层集合，从而把性能差异更直接地归因到方向可达性与方向效率。论文还分析了不同漂移区间下的稳定性，说明有些方向在小预算下就很高效，有些则需要更大漂移才显现收益，进一步支持“漂移大小”和“漂移方向”应分开讨论。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三处。第一，看引言和 Figure 1，理解作者为什么把 QA-only 微调当成检验方向选择的放大镜；这个设定决定了整篇论文的说服力。第二，看行为几何那一节，重点不是公式推导本身，而是“固定漂移预算后，只剩方向自由度”这个重写是否成立，以及作者如何让不同微调方法在同一坐标系下可比。第三，看 layer-selective probe 的实验图，尤其是 matched-drift 比较：如果不在相同漂移水平下比较，很容易把“走得更远”误判成“方向更好”。\n\n如果时间有限，优先看：Figure 1、方法部分关于 drift budget 与 directional efficiency 的定义、科学推理与翻译主表、层选择消融、以及 RL 初始化对比。预计 35–50 分钟可以抓住主线。若要深挖，建议重点想两个问题：一是行为漂移的度量是否足够稳健，是否会偏向某些输出风格变化；二是 layer-selective tuning 找到的好方向，究竟是在保护已有回路，还是在利用某些层的低可塑性做隐式正则。这两个问题决定它能否推广到更大模型和更复杂后训练场景。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks a sharper question than standard fine-tuning work: under a fixed behavioral drift budget from a reference instruct model, which update directions improve the target task most efficiently? The setting is deliberately hard. The starting model already relies on explicit reasoning trajectories, but fine-tuning uses QA-only supervision with no reasoning traces. In this regime, ordinary SFT often improves the supervised task while degrading reasoning and other existing capabilities. The paper tries to separate two possibilities: whether this failure is inevitable because the supervision is incomplete, or whether it is largely a direction-selection failure in optimization.","importance":"Its main value is conceptual but operationally useful. First, it reframes SFT around behavioral drift relative to a reference model, rather than around parameter distance or an added regularizer. That is closer to how practitioners actually experience model degradation. Second, it argues that at matched drift, the key difference between full fine-tuning, LoRA, and parameter-selective tuning is not how far they move, but which directions they can access. Third, it shows a nontrivial empirical point: QA-only fine-tuning does not have to destroy reasoning if the update subspace is chosen well. That matters for post-training recipes, continual adaptation, and RL initialization, because some failures usually blamed on weak supervision may instead come from poor update geometry.","technical_approach":"$5b","experiments":"$5c","reading_guide":"$5d","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"这篇不是预训练论文，但它把“能力退化”从经验现象改写成可控几何约束；若你在想 SFT/RL 如何反噬 base model，先看它怎么定义 drift，再决定要不要复现。","en":"Not a pretraining paper, but it turns capability regression into a constrained-geometry problem; if you care how SFT/RL feeds back into the base model, read the drift formulation first and only then decide whether it is worth reproducing."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Fei Yuan","Changjiang Gao","Yilei Tu","Yifeng Liu","Shujian Huang","Yu Qiao"],"notable_authors":["Shujian Huang","Yu Qiao"],"tags":["instruction-tuning","behavioral-drift","constrained-optimization","alignment","model-updates"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13680","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.13680","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.14708","title":"Lightning Weave: Improving the Accuracy-Efficiency Frontier of Reasoning Models through Capability Composition","abstract":"A core goal of efficient reasoning is to improve the accuracy-efficiency frontier. 学到后期会被错误梯度继续推走。这一点对任何离线蒸馏、缓存式 RLHF/RLAIF、甚至部分预训练数据重加权方法都有方法论价值。若关注 pretrain↔post-train 接口，这篇比一般 reasoning 蒸馏更值得看。","technical_approach":"$5e","experiments":"实验覆盖数学和代码两类推理场景，学生模型包括至少 Qwen3-4B 和 while aggressively shortening outputs can damage correctness. Instead of jointly optimizing for both objectives from scratch, the authors ask whether independently post-trained specialists already contain complementary capabilities that can be extracted and recomposed into a single student. The hard part is not generic multi-teacher distillation. It is how to represent each specialist’s contribution as a composable policy increment, and how to make offline on-policy distillation stable once supervision is cached.","importance":"This is mainly a post-training and reasoning-distillation paper rather than a pretraining one. Still, it has two useful implications for foundation-model research. First, it represents a learned capability as the policy shift induced by a post-training stage, measured relative to a pre-stage anchor checkpoint. That is a modular view of training increments, and it could matter for continual pretraining, mid-training annealing, or injecting RL-acquired behaviors back into a base model. Second, it directly tackles target drift in offline on-policy distillation. If cached trajectories are used with the wrong surrogate, the student can keep receiving non-zero gradients even after reaching the intended target policy. That lesson extends beyond reasoning distillation to cached preference optimization, offline RLHF-style pipelines, and other settings where supervision is precomputed.","technical_approach":"$5f","experiments":"$60","reading_guide":"For a skim, focus on two questions: how capability is represented, and why offline distillation drifts without an explicit target. Verifier-Gated Multi-Expert On-Policy Distillation for Scientific Reasoning","abstract":"Multi-teacher on-policy distillation (OPD) is becoming the standard way to integrate specialist capabilities into one model: train experts with RL, then distill them into the student on its own rollouts. Existing recipes assign supervision at the sequence level - each prompt goes to one domain teacher and every token receives the same weight - which implicitly assumes that a teacher is uniformly useful across a response. We find instead that useful teacher signal is sparse and heterogeneous alon","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T11:34:22Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15404.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章处理的是多教师 on-policy distillation 里的监督分配粒度过粗问题。现有做法通常在 prompt 或 domain 级别选一个 teacher，然后把整条回答的所有 token 都交给它监督，默认同一个专家在整段推理里都同样有用。作者指出这在科学推理里并不成立：一个专家可能只擅长计算，不擅长机理解释；同一道题里，不同 criterion 对应的最优教师也可能不同。因此真正的问题不是“给这道题选哪个老师”，而是“哪个老师该教哪一段 token”。","importance":"它和预训练的直接关系不强，更接近 post-train 阶段的 RL + distillation 配方改进。但其中有两个点对基座研究员仍有参考价值。第一，监督信号在序列内部往往是异质的，统一加权会把错误 teacher 信号扩散到整条轨迹，这和预训练里 token-level data quality / loss reweighting 的问题有相通之处。第二，文章把 verifier 从单纯的 reward source 变成了 teacher eligibility 的判据，这种“先判断谁有资格教，再决定教哪些 token”的思路，对多源蒸馏、mixture supervision、甚至 curriculum routing 都有启发。","technical_approach":"方法叫 VG-OPD，核心由两层选择组成：先做 criterion-level 的 expert eligibility，再做 token-level 的 supervision placement。\n\n先说 who teaches。每个 prompt 配一个 rubric，rubric 由若干 criterion、对应 verifier，以及权重组成。作者不满足于把这些 criterion 压成一个总 reward，而是保留其结构。对学生当前 rollout，以及每个候选 expert 在同一题上的答案，都用相同的 criterion verifier 打分。只有当某个 expert 在某个 criterion 上明确优于学生时，它才有资格在这个 criterion 上提供监督。这里的关键不是“这个 expert 在某个领域通常很强”，而是“它在当前样本、当前 criterion 上相对学生有可验证优势”。\n\n再说 which token to teach。即便 expert 在某个 criterion 上有资格教，也不是把整段回答都蒸馏过去，而是只在 expert 与 student 存在分歧的位置施加监督。作者把这种分歧位置构造成稀疏 mask，用来加权 token-level 的 distillation loss。这样做的直觉很直接：如果学生和专家在某段已经一致，就没必要再强行蒸馏；真正有信息增益的是 expert 改变学生局部决策的那些 token。\n\n训练上，VG-OPD 仍然嵌在 on-policy RL 框架里。基础 policy optimization 用的是 rubric-GRPO 一类方法：对学生自己的 rollout 按 rubric 得到任务奖励，再做 group-relative policy update。蒸馏项则以 token-level reverse KL 的单样本估计进入 advantage，但其权重不再恒为 1，而是由 verifier-gated eligibility 和 disagreement mask 共同决定。作者也明确指出，MOPD 这类 prompt-level domain routing 可以看成其特例：先验指定 teacher，且所有 token 等权。","experiments":"实验设置上，作者训练了 4 个 RL specialist expert，并在 4B 和 8B student 上评估，覆盖 7 个 benchmark，包含 scientific reasoning、domain science 和 general reasoning。摘要给出的主结果是：4B student 上，相比 rubric-GRPO，VG-OPD 在 scientific reasoning 平均准确率上提升 2.0 个点，在 domain science 上提升 3.3 个点；同时持续优于 coarse-grained 的 MOPD。8B student 也呈现相同趋势，并取得整套评测里最强的总体表现。\n\n更值得看的其实是消融。文章声称 verifier-based expert eligibility 和 selective token supervision 两部分都不可少，这意味着单独做 token 选择但不验证 expert 是否真的更强，或者只做 expert 选择却仍对全序列均匀蒸馏，效果都不够好。这个结论是合理的：前者解决的是“教哪里”，后者解决的是“谁有资格教”，两者缺一都会把错误监督引入学生轨迹。\n\n从方法评估角度，建议留意几类细节：一，criterion 数量和 verifier 质量对结果的敏感性；二，稀疏 mask 的覆盖率，是否真的只监督少量关键 token；三，不同 expert 在同一题内是否出现频繁切换，若有，说明方法确实学到了细粒度能力拼接，而不是换了个更复杂的 prompt-level router。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和 Preliminaries。作者的问题定义很清楚：sequence-level teacher assignment 为什么不够，criterion-level verifier 为什么能提供更细监督。然后直接看方法图和 loss 定义，重点理解两个 gating：eligibility gate 和 disagreement mask。最后看 ablation，确认收益到底来自 verifier 选师、token 稀疏监督，还是只是多加了几个 expert。\n\n预计 25 到 35 分钟能读完主线。若想判断它是否有更广泛价值，建议多看实验里关于不同 benchmark、不同 student 尺度、以及不同 teacher 组合的稳定性。如果 gains 主要集中在 rubric 完整、verifier 强的任务上，那它更像一种 verifier-rich setting 的专用 recipe；如果在 verifier 噪声下也稳，那对更一般的 RL distillation 会更有意义。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a granularity mismatch in multi-teacher on-policy distillation. Standard recipes choose one teacher per prompt or per domain and apply its supervision uniformly across the whole response. That assumes a teacher is equally useful everywhere in the reasoning trace. The paper argues this is false for scientific reasoning: one expert may help with calculation but hurt mechanistic explanation, and the best teacher can change across criteria within the same answer. The real problem is therefore not only which teacher for a prompt, but which teacher for which tokens.","importance":"This is mainly a post-training recipe rather than a pretraining contribution, but it carries two ideas that matter more broadly. First, supervision is heterogeneous within a sequence, and uniform weighting can spread bad signal across many tokens. That connects to token-level quality weighting and selective supervision in language model training. Second, the paper repurposes verifiers from reward providers into teacher-eligibility tests. That separation between deciding who is qualified to teach and where their signal should apply is a useful pattern for multi-source distillation and routing.","technical_approach":"$61","experiments":"$62","reading_guide":"For a skim, read the introduction and preliminaries first. The motivation is clearly stated there: sequence-level teacher assignment is too coarse, and rubric criteria already provide the structure needed for finer routing. Then inspect the method figure and the loss definitions, focusing on the two gates: eligibility and disagreement. Finally, go straight to the ablations to verify whether the gains come from the proposed routing logic rather than simply adding more experts.\n\nA 25 to 35 minute read should be enough for the main idea. For a deeper assessment, pay attention to robustness across benchmarks, student sizes, and teacher combinations. If the gains depend heavily on clean rubrics and strong verifiers, the method may be best viewed as a verifier-rich distillation recipe. We find that planting harmful but benign-sounding reasoning in the actor's context can steer it to perform adversarial actions while evading monitors, an attack we term \"plan injection\". We initially discover this attack in the multiple-choice question-answering monitorabil","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T18:33:27Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15989.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究的是 CoT monitoring 的一个结构性漏洞：如果有害推理不是模型自己在当前上下文里显式规划出来，而是以上游“计划”“提示”“建议”的形式被注入上下文，模型会不会直接采纳并改写成看似自然、无异常的自身推理，从而绕过监控。作者把这种攻击称为 plan injection。问题的关键不只是 prompt injection，而是 attribution failure：actor 会把外部注入的错误计划内化为自己的 reasoning trace，监控器看到的是一条表面干净的推理链，而不是明显的恶意意图。","importance":"对基座和对齐研究的意义在于，它直接削弱了“可见 CoT 可作为可靠监督与审计接口”的假设。很多安全方案默认只要让模型把思考过程写出来，再用另一个模型去审，就能发现危险规划、欺骗或不对齐行为。plan injection 说明这个接口并不稳：当有害计划已经在上游完成，actor 只是在下游复述并执行时，监控器可能几乎看不到可抓的异常信号。对预训练研究员，这个结果有两层启发。第一，模型对上下文中推理片段的来源归因很弱，容易把“读到的计划”当成“自己想出的计划”，这和长上下文训练、RAG、multi-agent、tool-augmented LM 的训练目标都有关。第二，增加 monitor 的推理 token 并不总是提升安全性，说明“更强 reasoning = 更强审计”不是单调成立的。它更像一个关于训练分布、归因能力和监督接口脆弱性的警告。","technical_approach":"方法上分成两部分：攻击发现与攻击泛化。\n\n攻击发现阶段，作者使用 investigator-agent elicitation framework，在已有的 monitorability hint setting 上自动搜索能诱导 actor 出错且不被 monitor 抓到的提示。这个 setting 原本是在多选题里附加一个指向错误答案的 hint，再看 actor 是否受其影响，以及 monitor 能否发现。作者发现 investigator 学到的不是简单给错误答案，而是注入一段错误但措辞温和、表面合理的推理计划。即便没有明确命令 actor 去遵循它，actor 也会在自己的 CoT 里复述、改写并沿着这条计划走到错误结论。\n\n泛化阶段，作者把这一观察抽象成 plan injection attack，并迁移到两类更难任务。第一类是 APPS coding backdoors，目标是在代码任务中诱导模型生成带后门或错误实现的方案。第二类是 bio/math disguised-answer injection，目标是在生物/数学推理中用伪装成正常分析的计划把模型带向错误答案。关键点在于，注入内容可以只是“可选计划建议”，不需要强制指令；模型仍会高概率采纳。\n\n监控评估方面，作者测试了 thinking monitors 和 non-thinking monitors。thinking monitor 指允许监控器自己展开推理；non-thinking monitor 更接近直接分类或短判断。论文发现前者平均更强，但并不稳健：在一些样本上，更多推理预算反而让 monitor 花更多 token 去合理化 actor 的错误推理，而不是识别其可疑之处。作者还测试了让 monitor 看到 injected plan 的情况，结果在 Bio-Math 任务上检测率最多下降 50%，说明额外上下文并不天然有利，反而可能把监控器一起带偏。\n\n和已有工作相比，这篇论文的区别在于：它不是要求 actor 主动隐藏恶意意图，也不是通过专门微调让 actor 学会规避监控，而是利用模型对上下文计划的顺从和归因缺失，让“脏计划”在“干净轨迹”里被执行。这使得监控器更难抓，因为可疑规划已经在上游完成，下游 trace 只剩下看似正常的推导。","experiments":"实验结果有几个关键数字。\n\n第一，攻击迁移性很强。作者最初是在一个 7B 模型上发现这种行为，之后表明它可以迁移到 8B 模型以及 671B 参数的 DeepSeek-R1-0528，说明规模增大并没有自然消除这一脆弱性。\n\n第二，plan injection 的行为诱导成功率很高。在 APPS 任务上，注入计划能在超过 80% 的尝试中把模型带向目标行为；在 bio/math 任务上，成功率超过 90%。这说明模型对“上下文中的现成计划”有很强的采纳倾向。\n\n第三，监控规避率并不低。作者报告在不同 monitorability benchmark 上，攻击后仍有 25%-33% 的样本可以逃过监控；若使用 non-thinking monitor，漏检率最高可达 76%。即便是最强 monitor，也至少漏掉 25% 的攻击样本。\n\n第四，更多监控资源不一定更好。论文给出案例表明，增加 thinking budget 后，monitor 可能把额外 token 用于补全和合理化错误推理，而不是提高警觉。另一个具体数字是，在 Bio-Math 任务中，如果让 monitor 直接看到 injected plan，检测率最多下降 50%。这点很重要，因为很多系统设计直觉上会认为“给 monitor 更多上下文总是更安全”。\n\n从实验设计看，论文覆盖了发现、迁移、规模扩展和 monitor 类型比较，结构比较完整。局限在于，它主要证明了 CoT 监控接口的脆弱性，还没有给出同样有力的防御方案或训练修复路径。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Introduction 和贡献列表，抓住攻击定义：不是普通 prompt injection，而是把错误计划伪装成 benign reasoning，让 actor 在 CoT 里无归因地接手。\n\n然后重点看攻击发现部分，尤其是 investigator agent 在 hint setting 中到底学到了什么样的提示。这里最值得注意的是：没有显式“请照做”的命令，模型仍会采纳计划。这说明问题不只是 instruction following，而是上下文推理片段的默认信任。\n\n接着看 APPS 和 Bio/Math 两组实验。前者说明代码场景里 plan injection 可以把错误设计嵌进实现；后者说明在 reasoning 任务里也能成立。建议重点看成功案例和失败案例各是什么样，哪些措辞最容易让 actor 内化。\n\n最后看 monitor 对比实验，尤其是 thinking budget 和“是否暴露 injected plan 给 monitor”两个变量。这两处最能改变对 CoT monitoring 的直觉。\n\n如果只是了解结论，20-30 分钟足够；如果想把它转化成训练或系统防御问题，建议细看附录中的 prompt 模板、监控器设置和案例 transcript。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a structural weakness in chain-of-thought monitoring. If the harmful reasoning is not explicitly generated by the actor in the current turn, but instead arrives as an upstream “plan,” “hint,” or “optional suggestion,” can the actor adopt it, paraphrase it as its own reasoning, and still produce a clean-looking trace that evades monitoring? The answer is yes. The key issue is not generic prompt injection alone, but attribution failure: the actor often treats externally supplied reasoning as if it were self-generated, leaving the monitor with a transcript that looks benign even when the underlying plan is harmful.","importance":"This matters because it weakens a central assumption behind CoT-based oversight: that visible reasoning traces are a reliable interface for auditing dangerous planning, deception, or misalignment. Plan injection shows that this interface is brittle when the harmful planning has already happened upstream and the actor merely inherits and executes it downstream. For pretraining and alignment researchers, there are two especially relevant implications. First, current models appear weak at source attribution for reasoning content in context. That has direct relevance to long-context training, RAG, tool-augmented LMs, and multi-agent systems. Second, giving the monitor more reasoning budget does not monotonically improve safety. separation，后者用于防止修复模块把被压制的有害方向又补回来。推理时，SPARK 不总是强制编辑所有 head，而是计算当前 KV 在有害子空间中的能量 E_h，并用闭式解得到每个 head 的混合系数 g_h*，在原始 KV 和 project-and-repair KV 之间插值。这个系数不是一个 learned harm detector，而是由表示中相关方向的表达强度决定。编辑只在 as","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T12:29:00Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15471.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"长链数学 RLVR 的奖励只有最终对错，但一段推理里不同区域对结果的贡献不一样。HISPO 处理的是 policy optimization 的 credit assignment 粒度问题：token 级重要性采样太细，容易受局部 ratio 噪声和长度归一化影响；sequence 高的区域通常对应模型不确定、分支选择或关键推理转折，HISPO 将这些信号用于划分 contiguous segments，并用 non-maximum suppression 避免边界过密或重叠。每个片段再根据 entropy-derived saliency 分配软权重。更新时不再逐 token 或整序列计算一个统一校正项，而是在片段粒度聚合 token likelihood ratio，并对片段 ratio 做 PPO/GRPO 风格的 clipping。它不引入 critic，不需要 step-level label，也不训练 process reward model；区别于 DHPO 这类 token/sequence 混合方法，HISPO 的主要设计点是显式形成 entropy-derived segment，并直接把 segment 当作 policy importance correction”这个问题切得比较准。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"HISPO targets credit assignment in long-form RLVR. Token-level correction, as in GRPO or DAPO, can be noisy and length-sensitive. Sequence-level correction, as in GSPO, is more stable but treats a heterogeneous trace as one update unit.","importance":"The paper is not a core pretraining recipe paper, but it is useful for the pretrain–posttrain interface. It proposes a middle-granularity update unit for verifier-only reasoning training. The main lesson is that intermediate structure in reasoning traces can be extracted from the model’s own uncertainty, without process labels, a value model, or a process reward model. This is relevant for long-reasoning RL, RL-style pretraining, and reward designs where only final correctness is available.","technical_approach":"The method builds contiguous segments from rollout-time token entropy. High-entropy regions are used as signals for uncertainty, branching, or possible reasoning transitions. Non-maximum suppression is used to avoid overly dense or redundant boundaries, and each segment receives a soft entropy-derived saliency weight. Policy optimization is then performed at the segment level: token likelihood ratios are aggregated within a segment, clipped at that granularity, and weighted by the segment saliency. Compared with token-level GRPO/DAPO, this reduces exposure to local ratio noise. Compared with sequence-level GSPO, it preserves some internal structure of the completion. The method does not require a critic, step labels, or a process reward model.","experiments":"The experiments fine-tune Qwen3-1.7B-Base with LoRA in a mathematical RLVR setup, comparing against GRPO, DAPO, and GSPO. On six math reasoning benchmarks, the selected-checkpoint evaluation reports higher Pass@8 than the strongest baseline on all six benchmarks, and Acc@8 matching or exceeding the strongest baseline on five of six. Because of compute limits, checkpoint selection and training diagnostics use Acc@4 and Pass@4. The reported diagnostics and boundary-sensitivity analysis suggest that the gains are more visible in multi-sample success rates than in single-sample accuracy, which points to better coverage or more stable updates on important reasoning regions.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 25–35 minutes. Focus on the motivation comparing GRPO, DAPO, and GSPO; then read the segment-construction procedure, the non-maximum suppression step, the soft weighting scheme, and the segment-level clipped objective. In the experiments, prioritize the Pass@8/Acc@8 table, the Acc@4/Pass@4 monitoring curves, and the segment-boundary sensitivity analysis. The main caveat is scale: the evidence comes from a 1.7B LoRA mathematical RLVR setting, so the design idea is more important than the exact performance numbers.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看 entropy 分段和对 GRPO/GSPO 的 ablation；若增益主要来自调参，不应过度外推。","en":"Read the entropy segmentation and GRPO/GSPO ablations; if gains come from tuning, do not overgeneralize."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Quoc-Vinh Lai-Dang","Hyo-Sang Shin"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLVR","policy-optimization","credit-assignment","GRPO","mathematical-reasoning"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15471","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.15471","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.13425","title":"ReCAST: Reward Credit Assignment across Timesteps for Online Diffusion Reinforcement","abstract":"Training diffusion models with multiple rewards requires distinguishing user preference from reward informativeness. User preference determines how much each reward should contribute to the overall objective; reward informativeness determines when its feedback is useful during denoising. Some rewards can meaningfully evaluate a sample as soon as global structure emerges, but others become informative only when the sample is nearly clean. To address both questions jointly, we propose ReCAST (Rewa","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-11T18:41:09Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13425.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"在多奖励扩散模型微调中，如何为不同奖励信号在去噪轨迹的不同时间步上进行合理的权重分配（Credit Assignment），以解决不同奖励在不同生成阶段信息量差异的问题。","importance":"首次在扩散模型的奖励微调中引入了“按奖励、按时间步”的信用分配机制。打破了传统静态标量化（Static Scalarization）的局限，为多奖励对齐提供了一种更精细、更符合扩散模型生成动态的优化范式。","technical_approach":"提出 ReCAST 方法。核心思想是：不同奖励（如整体布局 vs. 局部细节）在去噪过程的不同阶段才具有区分度。方法首先通过计算正向策略与当前策略在各个时间步的 Rényi 散度，衡量每个奖励在不同时间步的“区分度增益”（Discriminability Gain）。然后，将这些增益转化为一个权重矩阵，并通过投影操作确保矩阵满足两个边缘约束：行和等于用户设定的奖励预算（反映用户偏好），列和等于均匀的时间步权重（防止某个时间步被过度优化或忽略）。","experiments":"论文在摘要和引言中未提供具体的实验数据，但明确指出了该方法旨在解决多奖励微调中的权重分配问题，并预期在多目标对齐任务中取得优于静态权重的效果。","reading_guide":"泛读建议：重点理解引言中关于“静态标量化缺陷”的论述，以及第 3 节中如何通过 Rényi 散度计算区分度增益并构建权重矩阵的数学推导。预计阅读时间 15-20 分钟。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"How to properly allocate weights (Credit Assignment) for different reward signals across different timesteps in the denoising trajectory during multi-reward diffusion model fine-tuning, addressing the varying informativeness of different rewards at different generation stages.","importance":"Introduces the first 'per-reward, timestep-dependent' credit assignment mechanism in diffusion reward fine-tuning. It breaks the limitations of traditional static scalarization, providing a finer-grained optimization paradigm for multi-reward alignment that better matches the generative dynamics of diffusion models.","technical_approach":"Proposes the ReCAST method. The core idea is that different rewards (e.g., global layout vs. local details) become discriminative at different stages of the denoising process. The method first measures the 'discriminability gain' of each reward at different timesteps by calculating the Rényi divergence between the positive policy and the current policy. Then, it converts these gains into a weight matrix and uses a projection operation to ensure the matrix satisfies two marginal constraints: row sums equal the user-specified reward budget (reflecting user preference), and column sums equal uniform timestep weights (preventing over-optimization or starvation of any timestep).","experiments":"The abstract and introduction do not provide specific experimental data, but clearly state that the method aims to solve the weight allocation problem in multi-reward fine-tuning and is expected to outperform static weighting in multi-objective alignment tasks.","reading_guide":"Skim reading suggestion: Focus on the discussion of the 'defects of static scalarization' in the Introduction, and the mathematical derivation of calculating discriminability gain via Rényi divergence and constructing the weight matrix in Section 3. This poses a challenge when the desired action distribution is defined only implicitly by a Q-function, since directly sampling actions from the resulting distribution is generally intractable. We propose Refinement-Based Flow Policy Optimization (RFPO), a novel framework for training a flow policy in online reinforce","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T06:56:33Z","category_id":"post_train","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15123.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是一个很具体的训练难题：在连续动作在线强化学习里，如果策略用 flow model 表示，那么标准 flow matching 需要目标分布的样本对来训练；但 actor-critic 场景下，理想动作分布通常只通过 Q 函数隐式给出，形如 Boltzmann policy，无法直接采样。论文要做的，是在“目标分布不可直接采样”的条件下，仍然把 flow policy 稳定地往高价值动作分布上推。","importance":"它对预训练的直接相关性有限，因为主场景是在线 RL，而不是基座模型预训练。但方法层面有两个启发点。第一，它把 flow matching、energy-based target、MCMC refinement 组合成一个闭环，这和生成模型里“模型样本先自举，再用更强目标修正”的思路相通。第二，它讨论了当目标分布只能通过打分函数或能量函数间接定义时，如何训练可采样的生成器，这对 RLHF/RL、reward model 引导生成、以及某些非自回归生成目标都有借鉴意义。贡献量级更像一个扎实的方法论文，而不是会改变预训练主线的工作。","technical_approach":"RFPO 的核心结构是两步交替。\n\n第一步是 Q-guided sample refinement。给定状态 s，当前 flow policy 从高斯噪声 x0 生成动作 a。这个动作不是直接拿去做策略更新，而是作为 MCMC 的初值，用 MALA（Metropolis-adjusted Langevin algorithm）做有限步 refinement，目标分布是由 Q 函数诱导的能量分布 p(a|s) ∝ exp(Q(s,a)/alpha)。这里的关键设计是：作者不尝试直接从这个 Boltzmann 分布采样，也不直接回归 critic 导出的速度场，而是先让当前策略产生一个可行样本，再用 critic 梯度把它往高概率区域推，并用 Metropolis-Hastings 校正减少偏差。\n\n第二步是 self-target flow matching。refinement 后得到的动作 a' 与生成原始动作时使用的 base noise x0 配对，作为 conditional flow matching 的 source-target 对，训练 flow policy 学会把同样的噪声映射到更好的动作。这样做的含义是：目标样本不是外部 oracle 提供的，而是“当前策略样本 + 有限步 MCMC 修正”得到的自举目标。随着迭代进行，策略分布逐步逼近由 Q 定义的能量分布。\n\n和已有方法相比，区别主要在三点。其一，不是用 Q-weighted regression 或 advantage-weighted imitation 直接重加权已有动作，而是显式做样本 refinement。其二，不是训练 score model 去近似目标分布梯度，而是把 MCMC 作为中间算子，再用 flow model 摊销这个修正过程。其三，论文给了理想化分布动力学分析，说明在精确条件下该交替过程会单调提升最大熵目标，并以目标能量分布为不动点。这部分理论不是完整现实保证，但至少解释了为什么“refine 再 distill”是合理的。","experiments":"实验分两部分。第一部分是合成分布拟合，用来验证 RFPO 能否保留多峰结构。作者声称它能逼近几何形状复杂、带多模态的目标分布，这一点很重要，因为 Gaussian actor 往往在这里表达力不足，而很多 RL 任务的高价值动作本来就可能多峰。第二部分是连续控制在线 RL，对比标准 Gaussian-policy baseline，论文结论是 RFPO 在几乎所有任务上持平或更好。\n\n从全文片段能确认的关键实验信息包括：\n1. 目标策略采用由 Q/alpha 定义的 Boltzmann 分布；alpha 控制探索与集中程度。\n2. refinement 使用有限步 MALA，而不是长链采样，这意味着方法强调实用性而非精确采样。\n3. 论文不仅给最终回报，还关注策略分布是否能逼近目标分布，这比单纯看 return 更能说明 flow policy 的作用。\n\n当前给出的正文片段没有完整结果表，因此不能可靠填写具体环境名、网络宽度、MALA 步数、回报提升百分比等数字。精读时建议重点核对三类消融：MALA 步数对性能和稳定性的影响；去掉 MH 校正后是否退化；以及与直接 Q-guided flow matching、Gaussian SAC/actor-critic 的差异。如果这些消融充分，方法可信度会高很多。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看方法总览图和算法伪代码，理解“sample -> refine -> distill”这个闭环。然后看理论部分里关于理想化分布更新的命题，重点不是证明细节，而是弄清楚单调改进成立依赖哪些假设。最后看两组实验：合成多模态分布和连续控制任务。前者回答表达力问题，后者回答 RL 实用性问题。\n\n预计阅读时间 25–35 分钟。若时间更少，只看 Introduction、方法部分中 MALA refinement + self-target FM 的定义、以及主要结果表和 ablation。对做预训练的人来说，最值得借鉴的是“用有限步外部修正器产生更优目标，再用生成模型摊销”这一训练模式，而不是 RL 细节本身。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper addresses a concrete training bottleneck for flow-based policies in online continuous-control RL. Standard flow matching requires samples from the target distribution, but in actor-critic settings the desired action distribution is usually only defined implicitly through a Q-function, typically as a Boltzmann distribution. Since direct sampling from that distribution is intractable, the central problem is how to train a flow policy to move toward a Q-induced target without having direct target samples.","importance":"Its direct relevance to foundation-model pretraining is limited, since the setting is online RL rather than large-scale language modeling. Still, the method is interesting for two reasons. First, it combines flow matching, energy-based targets, and MCMC refinement into a practical bootstrap loop. Second, it studies how to train a tractable generator when the target distribution is only available through an energy or scoring function. The authors separate that question from the broader issue of what the model understands. Their central claim is that refusal does not broadly consult the model’s moral knowledge. That has direct implications for the pretrain–posttrain interface. The Capacity of Reasoning by Superposition","abstract":"Large language models solve hard problems through intermediate computations across multi-step reasoning. Traditional chain-of-thought encodes these computations as tokens. Recent continuous and recurrent methods instead move partial computations into fixed-dimensional latent states, where a single thought can superpose multiple alternatives. An intuitive approach discards past computations a","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-12T06:35:15Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13747.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文研究连续潜在推理里的一个基础容量问题：当模型把中间计算写进固定维度的连续向量，而不是显式输出成 token 时，这个向量到底应该保留“当前前沿状态”还是“累计历史状态”。直觉上，向一个归一化向量里塞更多对象会让每个对象的系数更弱、检索更难，所以似乎应该只保留当前 frontier。作者要回答的是，这个直觉在 reasoning by superposition 场景下是否成立。论文的结论是，很多情况下恰好相反：累计式 superposition 虽然存了更多对象，但在相同下游计算下反而需要更小的隐藏维度，因而容量更高。","importance":"它对基座研究的意义不在于立刻给出一个可直接替换 Transformer 的训练 recipe，而在于把“latent reasoning 的容量”从经验现象推进到可分析对象。近来连续 CoT、recurrent/looped Transformer、superposition reasoning 都在尝试把部分推理搬到隐状态里，但设计上常缺一个清楚原则：有限宽度下，隐状态该保留什么。论文给出的回答是，容量不只由支持集大小决定，更由被保留的信息是否能支持未来计算决定。这个判断对 recurrent LM、test-time latent reasoning、memory writing 机制、甚至 residual stream 中历史信息应保留多少，都有直接启发。尤其是它把“保留更多历史可能更省维度”讲成了一个可证明的条件，而不是经验猜测。","technical_approach":"作者构造了三个可比较的 superposition 结构。frontier superposition 只编码当前步活跃的候选；cumulative superposition 编码截至当前已到达的全部历史；general weighted superposition 则允许对历史中不同对象分配任意权重。三者共享同一个图结构、同一个候选 bank、同一个查询解码器，因此比较只落在“状态保留什么”这一点上。\n\n理论核心是 critical dimension 分析。每个对象有独立高斯嵌入，状态向量是这些对象嵌入的归一化叠加；下游查询通过同一解码器去区分 useful direction 与 competing directions。作者推导了有限维下的精确 score law，并据此比较不同 superposition 在给定查询类上的最小可用维度。关键结论是：对于一大类 state-independent、nonlocalized、bounded-influence 的查询，累计式 superposition 的历史项会对未来查询产生相干增益，而无关项主要表现为随机干扰，因此虽然支持集更大，信号并不会按“对象数增加”那样简单稀释。换句话说，真正决定容量的是未来计算如何重用这些历史分量，而不是当前存了多少个对象。\n\n论文还把这个抽象结论映射回 Transformer 机制。attention 负责扩展当前计算，residual writing 决定旧信息是被覆盖还是保留。对一般加权 superposition，最弱保留系数会成为后续 attention 能否重新路由到该记忆的瓶颈。如果把权重过度集中在少数对象上，其他历史对象虽然理论上还在状态里，但系数太弱，后续层几乎无法再利用。由此作者提出一个设计原则：当未来查询未知时，均匀的 cumulative weighting 是 minimax 的；它不是针对某个特定查询最优，而是在最坏情况下最稳健。","experiments":"实验分三层。第一层是理论验证型模拟，检查 finite-dimensional score law 与 critical dimension 预测是否和实际检索/判别成功率一致。第二层是 width scaling，对比 frontier、cumulative 和不同 weighted superposition 在隐藏维度变化时的成功曲线。核心结果是，在作者定义的非局部查询类上，cumulative 方案在更小维度下就能达到可用性能，而 frontier 需要更宽的状态。第三层是 token-level/模型内实验，作者在 lightweight 模型和 GPT-2 风格模型里实现 attention–residual 构造，验证“最弱保留系数是路由瓶颈”这一机制判断。\n\n从摘要和正文可见，论文更强调结构性结果而不是单一 benchmark 数字。值得抓的实验信号有两个：一是 cumulative 优势并不依赖查询完全对称或简单加和，作者允许查询是连续的、相关的、非对称的、identity-dependent；二是 weighted superposition 的失败模式很清楚——不是总能量不够，而是某些未来可能有用的历史项被分到过小权重，导致后续无法可靠读出。若从模型设计角度看，这比“平均性能提升多少”更重要，因为它直接对应 memory write policy 的取舍。","reading_guide":"泛读时先看 Setup 和三种 superposition 的定义，弄清楚作者比较的是状态保留策略，而不是换了任务或解码器。然后看 critical-dimension 理论部分，重点抓两个概念：nonlocalized query class 为什么允许累计历史产生相干增益；以及 finite-dimensional score law 如何把“支持集更大”与“可恢复性更强”这两个看似矛盾的现象统一起来。最后看 Transformer mechanism 那节，那里把抽象结论落到 attention + residual 的具体实现上，是整篇论文和实际模型设计连接最紧的地方。\n\n如果时间有限，建议顺序是：引言、三种状态定义、主定理与图示、weighted superposition 的 minimax 结论、GPT-2/token-level 实验。预计 30–45 分钟能抓住主线。深挖时可以重点想三件事：一，这里的查询类与真实 LLM 推理中的 next-step computation 有多接近；二，结论是否依赖独立高斯嵌入假设，真实 residual stream 中相关性更强时会怎样；三，若把这个原则用于 recurrent LM 或 latent CoT，训练时应如何鼓励“均匀保留历史”而不让状态被无关信息淹没。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper studies a basic capacity question for latent reasoning: when intermediate computation is stored in a fixed-width continuous state rather than written out as tokens, should that state preserve only the current frontier or the accumulated history? The usual intuition says that packing more objects into one normalized vector should dilute each coefficient and make retrieval harder, so frontier-only storage should be better. The paper shows that this intuition can fail in exactly the reasoning regimes people care about. Under a broad class of downstream computations, cumulative superposition can require fewer dimensions than frontier superposition even though it stores strictly more objects.","importance":"Its contribution is mainly foundational. Recent work on continuous CoT, recurrent or looped Transformers, and reasoning by superposition has shown that latent reasoning is possible, but design principles for what the latent state should retain are still weak. This paper gives a concrete answer: capacity is not determined by support size alone; it depends on whether the retained information is useful for future computation. That matters for recurrent LMs, latent scratchpads, memory-writing policies, and residual-stream design. The interesting part is not just the claim that history can help, but that this can be formalized as a lower critical-dimension requirement under explicit query classes.","technical_approach":"$6f","experiments":"$70","reading_guide":"$71","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"只看容量推导和实验设置；若证明干净，会修正 latent CoT 里“丢历史省容量”的默认设计。","en":"Read the capacity argument and setup; if the proof is clean, it weakens the default latent-CoT instinct to discard history."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Hongyu Gu","Chang Liu","Jingwen Fu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["latent-reasoning","superposition","chain-of-thought","mechanistic-analysis"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13747","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.13747","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.14011","title":"Convergent Emergence of In-Context Learning Across Modalities","abstract":"Few-shot in-context learning (ICL), the capacity of a model to infer abstract patterns from input-output examples provided in its prompt and apply them to new inputs, has been extensively studied in large language models trained for next-token prediction on human text. control 做对照：clean prompt 保留正确的输入-输出配对，deranged prompt 保留输入、输出、分隔符等表面统计，但打乱或破坏配对关系。两者性能差，即论文中的 clean-minus-deranged effect，用来估计模型是否真的从 demonstration 中推断映射，而不是只利用输出先验或局部格式。作者还比较每个抽象任务在不同模态上的 paired-mapping benefit，并计算跨模态相关性，以检验是否存在共同的 ICL 难度谱。这个设计比单独报告 few-shot accuracy 更有信息量，因为它控制了 tokenization、格式熟悉度、边际分布和预训练记忆等混杂因素。","experiments":"论文报告了几组主要现象。第一，多种模态中 performance 随 shot count 不是完全各自为政；同一类抽象任务在多个模态里倾向于同时容易或同时困难。图像模态相关性较弱，提示视觉编码或模型训练分布可能改变了这种共同结构。第三，few-shot 表现通常随模型规模增强，符合 ICL 是随预训练能力和容量逐步出现的现象。第四，作者也给出负例：chess 没有明显 paired-mapping effect，music 只在较高 shot The central question is whether this is evidence of training-data leakage or a misfire of internal induction and copying mechanisms.","importance":"The paper is useful as a mechanistic diagnostic for pretraining and instruction tuning. Prompt echoing can look like memorization, but the reported evidence points more strongly to context-triggered copying circuits. This matters for small instruct models and quantized deployments: some apparent alignment failures may come from low-level attention behavior interacting with chat-template and SFT distributions, rather than from high-level instruction-following alone.","technical_approach":"$72","experiments":"The study evaluates Qwen 3.5 4B, Llama 3.2 3B, Gemma 3n E4B IT, OLMo-2 0425 1B, OLMo-2 1124 13B, SmolLM2 1.7B, and several GGUF quantized variants. Dataset A and Dataset B each contain 384 prompts, and Dataset C contains 128 prompts. At temperature 1.0, each model-prompt pair is sampled 20 times with top_p=1.0, top_k=0, and a 160-token generation limit; temperature 0.0 and 0.3 are also tested. Echoing appears across all six model families. Lower temperature and greedy decoding do not remove the failure; in many model-dataset combinations they increase it. Quantization has mixed effects: SmolLM2 rises from 1.95% to 5.19%, while OLMo-2 1B slightly drops from 0.61% to 0.38%. The public-data overlap test suggests possible partial overlap but is too weak to explain the phenomenon. The forced-prefix and head-ablation results provide the main evidence: a small copied seed can make non-echoing prompts continue copying, and ablating high prefix-matching heads suppresses echoing more than random ablation.","reading_guide":"Skim time: about 25–35 minutes. It frames the failure as competition among latent keys: query keywords activate entity-specific associations learned during pretraining, and a misleading association can dominate explicit constraints in the prompt.","importance":"The main value for pretraining research is mechanistic. The paper links part of factual unreliability to pretraining-induced association strength, frequency imbalance, and competition among entity-keyword bindings. This helps explain why knowledge coverage alone is not sufficient. A model may store the right fact, but inference can still route through a stronger but irrelevant association. The result is relevant to data mixture design, entity-frequency balancing, repetition control, and conflict-aware corpus construction.","technical_approach":"The work first analyzes binding effects through targeted keyword perturbations. It shows that individual query terms can have large effects on answer selection, that these effects are tied to specific entities, and that exposure frequency in pretraining systematically shapes binding strength. When several bindings are present, their effects can be competitive and non-additive. The detection method then uses a two-stage perturbation procedure: identify an influential association, remove the corresponding keyword, and measure how the prediction reorganizes. Incorrect answers often show a recovery trajectory, where suppressing a misleading key reveals a previously masked correct key. Correct answers, when perturbed, more often become ambiguous rather than consistently recovering toward another answer.","experiments":"The paper reports experiments across multiple models and hallucination benchmarks, focusing on counterfactual changes in prediction behavior rather than only likelihood or self-consistency. It also proposes a taxonomy of hallucination regimes: knowledge-deficit errors, wrong-knowledge errors, context-distraction errors, and unstable-inference errors. Perturbation is most informative for context-distraction cases, uncertainty signals are better suited to unstable inference, and evidence-grounded verification is more useful when the model’s stored knowledge is unreliable. The supplied text does not include the full numerical tables or exact detection gains, so the safest reading is qualitative and mechanistic rather than performance-centric.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 30–45 minutes. Start with Section 2 on entity-keyword binding, especially the experiments tying binding strength to pretraining frequency and multi-keyword competition. Then read Section 3 to understand the two-stage perturbation detector and the notion of a recovery trajectory. Section 4 is useful as a diagnostic map for choosing hallucination detectors under different failure modes. Readers interested in pretraining data should pay particular attention to how frequency imbalance turns into inference-time misrouting.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"可读机制实验，不要只看检测指标；若频率和实体绑定结果稳，预训练数据分布对幻觉的解释要更细。","en":"Read the mechanism experiments, not just detection numbers; if the frequency and entity-binding effects hold, hallucination analysis needs a finer pretraining-data lens."},"metadata":{"institutions":["University of Wisconsin–Madison","Affiliation:University of Wisconsin–Madison"],"authors":["Xuhan Tong","Jiawei Zhang"],"notable_authors":["Jiawei Zhang"],"tags":["hallucination","mechanistic-analysis","keyword-perturbation","pretraining-frequency","entity-binding"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15106","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.15106","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.15654","title":"Empathy Is Steerable but Multi-Axial: Mechanism Geometry and Persona Effects in LLMs","abstract":"Activation steering has been used to control traits such as honesty, refusal, and sycophancy, yet supportive empathy is evaluated along multiple dimensions that need not correspond to independently controllable activation directions. LLM 内部，支持性同理心是否对应一个可独立操控的单一方向，还是由多个彼此纠缠的机制共同构成。作者用 EPITOME 的三维分解——情绪回应（ER）、解释性理解（IP）、探索性追问（EX）——把“同理心”拆成可操作的行为机制，然后用 activation steering 去做因果探针，而不是只看输出分数。第二个核心问题是 persona prompt 带来的同理心差异，是否能被这些机制方向解释；如果不能，说明 persona bias 并不是沿着一个简单的“更 empathic / less empathic”轴在移动。","importance":"对预训练研究本身，它不是直接改训练 recipe 的论文，但有两点启发很实在。第一，它说明高层行为属性在模型表征里未必是低维、可解耦的，这对很多“单向量控制一个 trait”的工作是个约束条件。若把这种观察往预训练侧推，意味着模型在学习社会性语言行为时，可能形成的是共享子空间而不是正交技能轴。第二，它把 persona-conditioned behavior variation 和内部机制表征分开了：输出上差异很大，不代表内部落在已知机制基底上。这对做 debiasing、steering、甚至 post-train alignment 都有提醒——如果偏差主要在机制基底之外，单向量修补通常不够。贡献量级更接近机制分析与 controllability 研究，而不是基础范式改写。","technical_approach":"方法上，作者在三类开源 instruct 模型上做了统一分析：Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct、Mistral-7B-Instruct。数据来自过滤后的 EPITOME peer-support 对话，先去掉过短样本和自伤相关样本，尽量减少上下文不足和安全模板干扰。每个机制维度都构造正负对比集合，再从残差流中提取 steering vector。核心做法是 contrastive activation addition：用高分与低分样本在某层表示的差向量作为候选方向，在推理时加到中间层残差流上，观察 ER/IP/EX 代理分数如何变化。\n\n技术重点不在“提出新 steering 算法”，而在三层分析框架。第一层是 layer sweep：逐层扫描干预位置，找跨模型都稳定有效的层。作者发现中间层最稳，文中重点分析 layer 15 附近的结果。第二层是 target / off-target intervention analysis：对 ER 向量干预时，不只看 ER 是否上升，也看 IP、EX 是否被连带拉动；IP、EX 同理。这样可以判断三者是否真能独立控制。第三层是 geometry + projection analysis：比较三个机制向量之间的夹角、子空间重叠，以及 persona prompt 引起的平均激活位移在 ER/IP/EX 子空间上的投影占比。这个设计很关键，因为它把“输出共变”与“内部共享表征”区分开了。\n\n和已有 activation steering 工作相比，区别主要有三点。其一，目标属性不是 honesty/refusal 这类较粗粒度单轴 trait，而是一个天然多维、带行为结构的社会性能力。其二，作者不满足于看 steering 是否能把分数调高，而是把 steering 当作表征几何探针，去问方向是否可分、子空间是否共享。其三，加入了 persona 条件下的机制投影分析，检验 prompt-induced bias 是否落在已恢复的机制基底内。这个问题设置比常见的“prompt A 比 prompt B 更 empathic”更深入。","experiments":"实验覆盖三类结果。第一，steering 可行性。作者在三种模型上都找到较稳定的中层干预点，contrastive activation addition 能一致地推动 EPITOME 代理分数变化，并且用第二个自动评估器复核后趋势仍在，说明不是单一 classifier artifact。文中还提到有一定 out-of-domain transfer，说明方向不是完全记住数据表面形式。\n\n第二，机制可分性。ER、IP、EX 三个方向并不独立。对某一机制方向做干预时，目标维度会上升，但非目标维度也会出现可测的 spillover。作者据此认为三者共享表征结构，而不是三条近似正交的控制轴。论文的主要结论不是“完全不可分”，而是“可区分但不可独立操控”：有方向性差异，但共享子空间明显，导致局部控制总会伴随副作用。\n\n第三，persona 效应。作者比较不同 persona prompt 下的激活变化，发现虽然 persona 能显著改变输出层面的 empathy score，但这些变化在已恢复的 ER/IP/EX 机制子空间中的投影很小。最醒目的数字是：在 layer 15，ER/IP/EX 子空间只能解释 persona-induced mean-squared activation shift 大约 3%。这意味着 persona 造成的同理心差异，大部分不在这组三机制基底里。换句话说，persona 不是简单地把模型沿“更多 ER / 更多 IP / 更多 EX”的方向推了一下，而是改动了更大、更复杂的表征结构。\n\n模型规模方面，实验都在 7B–8B instruct 模型上完成，没有更大闭源模型验证。评估主要依赖 EPITOME classifier 和一个 LLM judge，因此结论更适合理解为“机制代理层面的稳定现象”，而不是对人类感知同理心的最终定论。","reading_guide":"这篇适合泛读，但如果想抓住真正的信息量，建议先看 sharper question than “can LLMs be made more empathic.” It studies whether supportive empathy inside instruction-tuned LLMs is represented as a single controllable trait or as several partially entangled mechanisms. Using the EPITOME decomposition—Emotional Reactions (ER), Interpretations (IP), and Explorations (EX)—the authors treat activation steering as a causal probe of internal organization rather than a mere response-editing trick. A second question is whether persona-induced differences in empathic behavior can be explained by these recovered mechanism directions, or whether persona effects mostly live outside that basis.","importance":"This is not a pretraining-method paper, but it matters for foundation-model research in two concrete ways. First, it shows that a high-level social behavior may not correspond to a clean low-dimensional axis. That is a useful constraint on a large body of single-vector steering work. Pushed back toward pretraining, it suggests that socially grounded behaviors may emerge as overlapping subspaces rather than neatly factorized skills. Second, it separates output-level variation from mechanism-level variation: large persona-conditioned score changes do not imply movement along known internal mechanism directions. That matters for debiasing, controllability, and post-training alignment, because a simple vector intervention may miss most of the actual representational shift.","technical_approach":"$73","experiments":"$74","reading_guide":"$75","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"机制分析好文，证明了复杂概念（如共情）在 LLM 激活空间中是纠缠的，无法像简单特征那样完美正交解耦，对做 representation engineering 的人有参考价值。","en":"A solid mechanistic analysis showing that complex concepts like empathy are entangled in LLM activation space and cannot be perfectly orthogonally decoupled, useful for representation engineering."},"metadata":{"institutions":["Ajou University","Affiliation:Department of Artificial Intelligence, Ajou University"],"authors":["JuHeon Ha","Byounghan Lee","Yunseo Choi","Kyung-Ah Sohn"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["Activation Steering","Representation Engineering","Mechanistic Interpretability","Empathy"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15654","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.15654","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.15975","title":"Disentangling Representation Evolution in Transformers through Directional Decomposition","abstract":"Transformer representations evolve through learned additive transformations that either preserve their current direction or redirect it. We study this evolution as a functional geometry, decomposing learned updates into parallel and perpendicular components. Across pretrained models, we find substantial parallel components beyond the residual identity path. We then apply the decomposition in two spaces: to attention and MLP updates relative to the hidden state, and to attention value aggregation","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T17:56:19Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15975.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL","cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"Transformer 的残差更新里，沿输入表示方向的“平行分量”（只改长度不改方向）长期占很大比例；论文要回答它到底是功能必需，还是参数在做冗余的缩放，并进一步问：这种几何结构能否指导压缩质量诊断与预训练改进。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在提出新训练目标，而在给出一个可操作的“方向几何”视角：1）解释训练后模型为何会学出大量看似可由残差通路“免费”提供的缩放；2）给压缩/量化的误差度量提供更贴近行为的指标（垂直误差比平行误差更能预测质量）；3）更关键的是把该几何结论落到训练配方上：在预训练中抑制某些平行注意力分量，能稳定降低验证损失并提升下游表现，提示注意力里存在可被正则化掉的“方向保持型容量”。","technical_approach":"核心方法是把每个加性更新 Δ 相对其参考向量 z 做正交分解：Δ=Δ∥+Δ⟂。Δ∥ 等价于对 z 做标量缩放（残差加法后变成 (1+α)z），Δ⟂ 才负责把表示“转向”到新语义子空间。论文做了两套分解空间：\n1）Residual space：直接对每个子层（Attention/MLP）输出的残差更新，相对该子层输入表示分解，衡量网络在残差流里到底在做多少缩放 vs 转向。\n2）Attention value space：把注意力内部的 value 聚合（投影前）相对 token 自己的 value 向量分解，从而把“自值直通/保向”与“跨 token 聚合带来的转向”拆开。这个空间的动机是：残差空间里 attention 输出混合了多步线性变换，可能掩盖了注意力检索阶段的几何结构。\n\n在此基础上，论文用 component-scaling interventions 做因果测试：分别对 Δ∥ 或 Δ⟂ 乘以系数，观察困惑度/任务性能变化。结论是强烈的方向不对称：缩放垂直分量会迅速破坏行为；缩放平行分量在很宽区间内影响很小，且这种“平行鲁棒性”在 value space 更明显（尤其是跨 token 聚合部分的平行分量）。\n\n扩展到两类应用：\n- 压缩诊断：把量化/剪枝带来的更新畸变也分解为平行/垂直误差，发现更好的压缩模型往往对应更低的垂直误差；平行误差区分度弱。\n- 预训练配方：提出 parallel attention suppression（两种变体：残差空间抑制、value 空间抑制）。直觉是：既然平行分量对行为不敏感，训练时抑制它可能迫使容量转向更有用的垂直更新，或减少无效的尺度摆动，从而改善优化轨迹。","experiments":"全文实验主线包括：\n- 统计测量：在多种预训练模型上测 Δ∥/Δ⟂ 的相对幅度，展示平行分量在层间/步间持续存在（文中以 Qwen3 不同规模示例画出比值随生成步变化）。\n- 干预实验：对 residual space 与 value space 分别做平行/垂直缩放。总体现象是：垂直缩放更“脆”，平行缩放更“钝”；且 value space 的平行缩放最不敏感，说明注意力内部存在大量方向保持型聚合对最终行为贡献有限。\n- 压缩相关：在量化/剪枝模型上比较“平行误差 vs 垂直误差”与最终质量的相关性，垂直误差更能解释好坏差异。\n- 预训练相关：从 296M 到 2.7B 的规模做从头预训练对比，parallel attention suppression 的 value-space 版本在验证损失轨迹上更低；两种版本都带来下游提升，value-space 版本增益更大。原文摘要未给出具体提升数字；若需要复现或做二次分析，建议直接对照论文实验表格/曲线读取。","reading_guide":"泛读建议（约 30–45 分钟）：\n- 先看引言末尾对三条结论的表述，明确它不是纯机制分析，而是把结论落到压缩指标与预训练 recipe。\n- 重点看方法部分对 residual space vs attention value space 的定义差异：value space 分解为什么能把“自值流”与“跨 token 聚合”拆开，这是全文最关键的建模选择。\n- 干预实验的图表要对照看：同样是平行缩放，为什么 value space 更稳、residual space 更不稳；这决定了后面为什么 value-space suppression 更有效。\n- 若关心训练配方，直接跳到 parallel attention suppression 的实现细节与训练曲线；关注它到底是对哪些张量/路径做抑制、抑制强度如何设定、是否引入额外超参敏感性。\n- 若关心压缩，重点看“垂直误差更能预测质量”的相关性图/表，并思考它能否作为量化/剪枝的优化目标或 early stopping 信号。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"Transformer residual updates contain a surprisingly large component parallel to the incoming hidden state (direction-preserving scaling). The paper asks whether this parallel component is functionally necessary or largely redundant, and whether the resulting “directional geometry” can be exploited to better understand compression quality and to improve pretraining recipes.","importance":"The main contribution is a usable geometric lens rather than a new objective: (1) it provides evidence that direction-changing (perpendicular) updates are behavior-critical while direction-preserving (parallel) updates are broadly behavior-robust; (2) it turns this into a practical diagnostic for compression—perpendicular distortion tracks compressed-model quality better than parallel distortion; (3) it proposes a pretraining intervention (parallel attention suppression) that consistently improves validation-loss trajectories and downstream results across 296M–2.7B scales, suggesting there is trainable but low-utility capacity devoted to direction-preserving attention behavior.","technical_approach":"$76","experiments":"Empirical evidence is organized as:\n- Measurement: across pretrained models, quantify the magnitude ratio ||Δ_parallel||/||Δ_perp||, showing substantial parallel components persist across depth and generation steps (illustrated with Qwen3 variants).\n- Intervention: in both residual space and value space, independently scale parallel vs perpendicular components and observe that perpendicular edits break performance much faster; value-space parallel edits are the most behavior-preserving.\n- Compression analysis: across quantized/pruned models, better models correlate with lower perpendicular distortion; parallel distortion is less discriminative.\n- From-scratch pretraining: across 296M–2.7B, parallel attention suppression yields lower validation-loss curves; both variants improve downstream metrics, with the value-space variant showing the largest gains. Those might include a projection, a cosine, an ablation delta, or an interchange patch among others. These measurements fail in specific, diagnosable ways that return a plausible number instead of an error, so a broken instrument can easily read as a finding. reordered-n","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-13T19:37:02Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_14754.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG","cs.AI","cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇文章处理的不是某个具体机理结论，而是因果可解释性里一个更基础的问题：很多常用测量仪器会在失效时给出一个看起来很像发现的数值，而不是直接报错。作者围绕拒答机制研究，系统整理了六类这种“静默失效”模式：投影分数在低维决策瓶颈处失去判别力、内容位点的大激活离群被误读为决策位点污染、协方差匹配 层而变成伪结论。核心问题不是如何做出更花哨的解释，而是如何先校准仪器，确认测到的量有分辨率、有功效、且测在正确深度上。","importance":"对预训练研究的直接价值不在于拒答本身，而在于它把一套可迁移的测量纪律讲得很具体。现在很多训练动态、表示分析、对齐机理、甚至 tokenizer 或数据效应分析，都依赖投影、消融、patching、head attribution 这类工具。文章给出的结论是：如果不先做正控梯度、正交验证、功效估计和深度对齐，很多“机理发现”只是仪器读数。这个提醒对基座研究很重要，因为预训练阶段的表示往往更高维、更分布式，若在后训练形成的低维控制通道上都能出现如此严重的测量错觉，那么在更复杂的预训练表征上更需要先做 instrument calibration。贡献类型偏方法学与实验规范，量级上不是提出新模型，但对做 mechanistic plausible-looking numbers when they are broken, instead of failing loudly. Using a refusal-mechanism program as the running case, the authors document six such silent failures: projection-fraction metrics losing discriminative power at low-dimensional decision bottlenecks, large activation outliers at content positions being misread as contamination of the decision token, covariance-matched nulls saturating on massive-activation families, per-head OV attribution systematically overstating true residual writes on reordered-norm architectures, interchange patches becoming sign-chaotic when the measured outcome is pinned near a ceiling, and read-from claims turning into artifacts when measured after the model has already committed. The central question is not how to produce another mechanistic story, but how to calibrate the measurement stack before trusting any story it tells.","importance":"Its value for pretraining researchers is indirect but real. A large fraction of current work on training dynamics, representation analysis, alignment mechanisms, and post-training control relies on the same family of instruments: projections, ablations, patching, and attribution. The paper’s main contribution is to show that these tools can fail in structured, diagnosable ways, and that the failure can look exactly like a scientific result. That matters even more for pretraining analysis, where representations are typically higher-dimensional and more distributed than the narrow post-training control channels studied here. If instrument failure is this severe in a relatively clean low-dimensional setting, calibration becomes even more necessary when probing pretraining-native features. Recent developments in mechanistic interpretability suggest sparse autoencoders (SAEs) as a ","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T05:29:45Z","category_id":"mechanism","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15064.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.LG"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作研究 RL 后训练到底改变了模型内部表示的哪些部分：RL 是创造新特征，还是主要放大预训练阶段已经存在的特征与采样模式。","importance":"它对预训练研究的价值在于把 RL 的作用从行为指标拉回到表示层。结论指向一个很具体的假设：在数学推理 RL 中，性能提升很大一部分来自对既有表示的选择性激活，尤其是后层的格式、步骤边界和答案结构相关特征，而不是大规模重写问题内容表示。这个结论会影响 pretrain 与 posttrain 的接口设计：如果 RL 主要是在调用已有能力，那么预训练阶段能否形成可被奖励信号稳定调用的潜在特征，比单纯延长 RL 更关键。","technical_approach":"核心方法是 Fixed-SAE Track。普通 SAE 适合解释单个模型 checkpoint，但不同 checkpoint 独立训练 SAE 后，特征编号和方向不可直接比较。已有的 SAE Track 通过沿训练轨迹顺序微调 SAE 来跟踪特征，但会继承前一阶段字典，对新出现特征不够敏感。Fixed-SAE Track 改为在每个考察层训练一个共享 SAE，训练数据来自 base model 和所有 RL checkpoint 的激活混合。之后所有 checkpoint 都用同一个固定字典重新编码。这样每个 SAE latent 在训练前后有相同的方向，表示漂移可以用同一组可解释特征的激活率、强度和分布变化来度量。作者把该方法用于数学推理 RL，并进一步通过 feature steering 检验这些特征是否具有因果作用。","experiments":"主要发现有四点。第一，RL 引起的表示漂移整体较小、渐进，并集中在模型后层。第二，变化最明显的不是题目语义或问题内容特征，而是一小组 ladder tokens 和格式脚手架特征，例如步骤换行、推理分段、答案分隔符等。第三，把这些 RL 增强的特征直接 steering 到 base model 中，可以恢复约 80% 的 RL 性能增益，说明这些特征不只是相关信号，也参与了输出行为改变。第四，作者设计了一个目标特征已知的合成 benchmark，用来检验该框架能否识别真正新出现的特征。论文声称结果覆盖多个模型和不同训练设置，但提供内容未列出具体模型规模、数据量和完整分数表。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 45–60 分钟。重点看方法定义部分，尤其是共享 SAE 的训练数据如何从不同 checkpoint 池化、表示漂移指标如何定义、如何避免把 SAE 重构误差误判为模型变化。实验部分优先看三类结果：层间漂移分布、Top changed features 的人工解释、feature steering 恢复 RL 增益的因果验证。合成 benchmark 可以快速看，它主要用于确认方法能不能发现新特征。若关注 RL pretrain 或 RLVR，这篇的关键问题不是“RL 有没有用”，而是“RL 改的是哪些表示，以及这些表示在预训练阶段是否已经存在”。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks what reinforcement learning changes inside an LLM. In particular, it tests whether RL creates new internal features or mainly amplifies features already present in the pretrained model.","importance":"The contribution is useful for the pretrain–posttrain interface. It suggests that, at least in math-reasoning RL, much of the gain may come from eliciting existing late-layer features rather than rewriting the model’s content representation. This matters for pretraining: a base model needs to learn latent reasoning and formatting features that later reward signals can reliably select. It also gives a mechanistic explanation for why RL can improve benchmark behavior without necessarily expanding the model’s underlying capability boundary.","technical_approach":"The proposed method is Fixed-SAE Track. Standard sparse autoencoders decompose activations into interpretable sparse latents, but independently trained SAEs are not comparable across checkpoints because feature indices and directions are arbitrary. Sequential SAE tracking reduces this issue but inherits dictionaries along the trajectory, which can make emerging features harder to detect. Fixed-SAE Track trains one shared SAE per selected layer on activations pooled from the base model and all RL checkpoints. Every checkpoint is then re-encoded through this fixed dictionary. Because feature directions are held constant, changes in latent firing rates, activation magnitudes, and distributions become comparable across training. The method is applied to RL post-training for mathematical reasoning, and feature steering is used to test whether the changed features causally account for performance gains.","experiments":"The reported findings are specific and relatively sharp. RL-induced drift is small, gradual, and concentrated in late layers. The largest changes appear in a small set of ladder-token and formatting-scaffold features, such as step breaks and answer delimiters, rather than in features representing problem content. Steering these RL-enhanced features into the base model recovers around 80% of the RL performance gain, which supports a causal role for these features. The authors also introduce a synthetic benchmark with known target features to test whether the framework can detect genuinely novel feature emergence. The supplied content does not provide full model sizes, training budgets, or detailed score tables.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 45–60 minutes. Read the Fixed-SAE Track setup carefully: how activations are pooled across checkpoints, how the shared dictionary is trained, and how representation drift is quantified. Then focus on the layer-wise drift plots, the most changed SAE features, and the steering experiments that recover most of the RL gain. The synthetic benchmark is useful as a method sanity check. For researchers working on RLVR or RL-for-reasoning, the main point is to separate behavioral improvement from representational novelty.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"适合看方法设定，不要先信解释结论；固定 SAE 坐标系若干净，能给 RL-pretrain 接口一个可复现实验模板。","en":"Read the setup before trusting the interpretations; a fixed-SAE coordinate system could become a useful template for RL-to-representation audits."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Lingheng Du","Yiming Tang","Xufeng Duan","Dianbo Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["RLHF","mechanistic-interpretability","SAE","representation-dynamics"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.15064","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.15064","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.14127","title":"Exact Finite Attention Responses From RoPE Derivatives","abstract":"We derive exact local responses for attention interventions, allowing candidate edits to be scored from a cached baseline and one backward pass. The main cases are RoPE positional shifts and simultaneous key-value edits, where first-order attribution often misses important finite effects.","importance":"This is more of an analysis and intervention tool than a pretraining recipe. Its relevance lies in making attribution patching, cache editing, and long-context diagnostics more reliable. A positional Jacobian linearizes both the RoPE rotation and the softmax normalization; that approximation can be poor when the displacement is not tiny in high-frequency rotary bands. The proposed finite response can score edits from a cached baseline and a shared downstream gradient, giving a sharper instrument for studying attention behavior.","technical_approach":"The derivation starts from the continuous RoPE identity ∂p z(p)=A z(p). Instead of using only the tangent δ A z(p), it integrates the derivative to obtain the exact finite rotary displacement through φ1(B)=∫_0^1 exp(tB)dt. This handles zero-frequency blocks without needing A inverse and makes the error of the tangent approximation explicit by rotary band. The resulting score increments are then propagated through softmax with scalar divided differences, so the normalization change is retained rather than linearized. For joint key-value interventions, the output change is decomposed into attention reallocation, edited value content, and the interaction term C_KV = Σ_j (p'_j - p_j) ε_j. That term is exactly what separate key and value attribution leaves out. The paper also develops certificates based on KL error and a common-logit gauge for checking local ordering and feasible control directions.","experiments":"The positional-edit study executes 92,160 edits over 768 held-out prompt sets. The finite predictor reaches 95.36–96.52% sign accuracy on answer-margin changes. It reduces MAE by 73.6–82.5% relative to the positional Jacobian and by 36.2–50.9% relative to a zero-change baseline. In six joint-cache settings, keeping the C_KV interaction lowers downstream MAE by more than a factor of nine compared with separate attribution, with paired 95% intervals favoring the joint finite response in every setting. Frequency and strength sweeps show that the methods agree in the small-edit regime, while the finite correction becomes important as rotary displacement grows.","reading_guide":"Plan for a 30–45 minute skim. same-inst","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T16:47:02Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15851.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CL"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是一个很具体、也很现实的问题：模型在测试时确实能“从自己的历史轨迹里学到东西”，但原始轨迹往往太长、太脏、真假信息混杂，直接把整段历史喂回去并不能稳定提升下一次作答。作者想解决的不是如何继续微调 的失败和中间过程里提炼有效上下文。对预训练/后训练接口的意义在于，它提示了一种新型 credit assignment 方式：奖励不是给最终回答模型，而是给生成“经验摘要”的模型。若把这个思路往前推，可以连接到 trajectory distillation、agent memory、continual deployment learning，甚至把“经验抽取器”当成 mid-train 或 actor、Self-Refinement 和未训练 coach。论文强调两点结果。\n\n第一，text-games 上收益更大。原因不难理解：这类任务的轨迹里包含潜在环境规则、失败尝试和局部可验证线索，原始历史很冗长，训练过的 coach 更容易把“规则”抽出来；而未训练 coach 或直接 self-refine 很容易被噪声带偏。作者还指出，cross-instance reward 解码预算翻倍；也就是说，额外算力如果用在“提炼经验再重试”，比直接多采样/多解码更值。这一点对推理时 compute allocation 很有启发。\n\n从文中可确认的实验机制看，作者比较了 same-instance 与 cross-instance 两种训练信号，分析了 OOD transfer，并测试了 coach 对不同 actor 的适配性。摘要里没有给出完整数值表，提供的全文片段也未包含所有表格数字，因此不适合编造具体百分点；但可以确定的是，作者做了任务级对比、迭代次数分析、reward 变体比较、以及 actor-specific transfer 分析，这几部分构成了主要证据链。","reading_guide":"泛读时先抓三件事。\n\n第一，看方法部分对 reward 的定义，尤其是 same-instance 和 cross-instance 的差别。这里决定了 coach 学到的是“纠错提示”还是“可迁移规则”。如果只看一个点，这就是最该看的点。\n\n第二，看多轮更新公式和对应实验图。重点不是它能不能多轮，而是为什么“替换经验”比“累积历史”更合理，以及性能是否真的随轮数持续增长。这里关系到它是不是一种有效的 test-time scaling。\n\n第三，看分析部分关于 actor-specificity 和 OOD transfer。若 coach 只能记住训练集模板，价值有限；若它既能适配特定 actor，又能在分布外保留部分收益，说明它学到的是介于 prompt engineering 与 policy shaping 之间的东西。\n\n预计阅读时间 20–30 分钟。方法不复杂，主要价值在实验设计和问题拆法。读的时候要带着一个问题：这个 coach trajectory often contains useful evidence, failed attempts, and misleading reasoning all at once, so feeding the raw trajectory back to the same model is an unstable way to improve the next attempt. The goal is not to further tune the actor itself, but to train a separate coach model that converts a frozen actor’s trajectory into compact, actionable experiential knowledge. The paper further separates two targets: knowledge that helps on the same instance, and knowledge that transfers across related instances.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model research is conceptual rather than benchmark-driven. That creates a different credit-assignment path: reward the model that writes the experience summary, not the model that produces the final answer. This is relevant to test-time scaling, agent memory, trajectory distillation, and the pretrain↔post-train interface. We present Realtime-Venus, a proactive full-duplex interaction system with two separately trained 9B models: Realtime-Venus-Omni for audio-visual interaction and Realtime-Venus-Audio for spoken interaction. Each model serves as a complete co","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-12T08:56:50Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13814.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.CV","eess.AS"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作解决的是实时音视频对话中的时间尺度错配问题：前端需要连续听、看、说，并在用户插话或纠正时即时调整；后台推理、工具调用或长任务又需要稳定的请求边界和证据快照。难点不在单次回答质量，而在同一会话里把全双工交互、主动发言、原生语音生成和异步委托组织到一个可训练、可执行的时间线中。","importance":"对基座预训练研究的直接贡献有限，更接近 omni 模型的后训练数据、交互协议和系统接口设计。但它有参考价值：它把用户音视频流、模型语音/文本输出、交互控制 token、私有委托请求和后台结果放到共享因果时间线上，这对训练 native speech / audio-visual LM 很有启发。相比只把工具调用当作文本函数调用，它强调工具请求必须绑定生成时刻的证据快照，并在结果返回时重新放入已经变化的对话上下文。","technical_approach":"系统包含两个独立训练的 9B 前端模型：Realtime-Venus-Omni 面向音视频交互，Realtime-Venus-Audio 面向纯语音交互，均建立在 MiniCPM-o 4.5 之上。前端模型在流式序列中联合预测交互控制 token、回复文本和私有委托请求，因而可以学习何时继续说、何时停下、何时忽略用户 backchannel、何时根据纠正修改未说出口的后续内容。运行时采用 dual-loop：前端维持实时对话，Realtime-Venus-Harness 在后台执行已注册能力。每个委托请求会绑定原始 session，并保存发起时可见的证据快照；后台结果以私有上下文形式返回，经过 freshness check 和 playback-aware delivery 后再交给前端决定是否、何时、如何说给用户。训练数据管线由 scenario planning、speech realization 和 temporal alignment 组成，显式区分 backchannel、他向发言、真正打断、修正和重定向，并把主动发言和异步委托轨迹混入同一时间线。","experiments":"论文报告了理解能力、对话连续性和委托能力三类评测。Realtime-Venus-Omni 在所评估的在线模型中，在 8 个视频 benchmark 里的 6 个取得领先；Realtime-Venus-Audio 在若干音频理解和 spoken QA 比较中取得最高分。全双工评测显示，在非打断式用户发声下模型有较高 continuation rate，说明它能较好地区分 backchannel 和真正插话。工具使用和委托决策评测把“是否正确路由任务”和“后台任务是否成功完成”分开看，这一点比较实用，也暴露出实时会话中外部执行仍然是主要瓶颈之一。给出的材料没有包含更细的表格数值。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20–30 分钟。优先看 Introduction 中对时间尺度错配的定义，以及统一 streaming formulation、Realtime-Venus-Harness 和数据管线部分。重点不是 benchmark 分数，而是三个设计点：一是把控制、回复和委托放到同一 causal timeline；二是委托请求绑定证据快照，而不是读取不断变化的会话状态；三是训练轨迹如何标注 backchannel、interrupt、repair、delegate 和 returned result。做语音 LM、omni LM 或工具增强实时交互的人可以细看数据构造方案。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a practical mismatch in real-time audio-visual dialogue. The central issue is not single-turn answer quality, but how to train and run a full-duplex multimodal conversational model that can delegate work asynchronously without losing conversational coherence.","importance":"This is not a core pretraining paper. Restarting repeats completed work, but continuing from unverified or outdated progress can carry earlier errors forward. A saved state is not necessarily a suitable place to resume. We introduce recoverability as a system primitive that makes reuse an explicit decision: select a supported starting point and a permitted recovery action, or withhold automatic continuation. Its behavioral contract bin","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-12T02:54:33Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13672.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论长程 AI agent 被中断或失败后，系统应当从哪个已保存状态恢复、允许执行什么恢复动作，以及什么时候不应自动继续。","importance":"它不是预训练论文，但对通用 agent 系统和 agent evaluation 有启发。长程任务的最终成功率会掩盖错误恢复：agent 可能从不该复用的 checkpoint 继续，最后仍然完成任务。论文把 recoverability 作为可检查的系统原语，有助于设计更可靠的 agent 运行时，也能为长程 agent 训练数据和评测协议提供约束。","technical_approach":"论文把恢复拆成三个对象：continuation source，即可复用的保存状态；recovery route，即从该状态出发允许执行的恢复动作；continuation authority，即在故障事件发生时，系统是否有证据和策略允许这个状态-动作组合被执行。这里的关键点是，checkpoint 被保存并不等于它适合恢复；某个动作可重试也不等于它能从任意状态重试。系统需要同时检查保存状态、验证记录、外部副作用、策略限制和过期条件。\n\n形式化部分把执行轨迹表示为包含 operational state、artifact versions、retained claims、execution history、stored checkpoints 和 validation records 的状态。参考架构包括 candidate management、evidence assessment、decision、execution 和 audit。它不生成修复内容，而是在控制平面上决定是否允许继续执行，并保留可审计证据。","experiments":"实验包括 4 个 deterministic challenges 和 20 个 paired file challenges。结果展示了几个容易被普通评测忽略的问题：准确恢复字节级 checkpoint 不代表恢复点被策略允许；最终任务成功也可能掩盖错误的 continuation source；执行了某个恢复动作并不代表它在当时拥有 continuation authority。论文还通过 progress controls 区分了真正复用已验证进度和重复完成任务之间的差异，并用 authorization tests 区分执行决策与事后策略检查。","reading_guide":"建议泛读，预计 20 到 30 分钟。重点看 introduction 中的文档编辑例子，能快速理解为什么最新 checkpoint 不一定是正确恢复点。然后看 problem formulation，关注 S、A、M、H、C、V 这些状态成分如何刻画 agent 执行。最后看实验设计，而不是只看最终结果；这篇论文的价值在于把恢复错误变成可观测、可测试的系统行为。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper asks how a long-horizon AI agent should resume after interruption or failure: which saved state can be reused, which recovery action is allowed, and when automatic continuation should be withheld.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper, but it is relevant to general agent systems and agent evaluation. Final task success can hide an invalid recovery decision. An agent may resume from a checkpoint that should not have been reused, repair the task later, and still look successful under ordinary end-to-end metrics. Treating recoverability as an explicit system primitive makes recovery decisions auditable and testable. This is useful for long-horizon agent runtimes, training trace quality, and evaluation protocols.","technical_approach":"The paper separates recovery into three concepts. The continuation source is the saved state selected for reuse. The recovery route is the corrective action to run from that state. Continuation authority is the permission, granted at the failure event, to execute that particular source-route pair under evidence and policy. A saved checkpoint is therefore not automatically a valid recovery point, and a retry rule is not automatically valid from every state.\n\nThe formulation models an execution trajectory with operational state, artifact versions, retained claims, execution history, stored checkpoints, and validation records. This allows the system to represent disagreements: memory may refer to an old artifact, a checkpoint may lack validation, or a tool call may have external side effects. The reference architecture connects candidate management, evidence assessment, decision making, execution, and audit. It does not produce the repair itself; it controls whether a proposed recovery transition is authorized.","experiments":"The experiments use four deterministic challenges and 20 paired file challenges. They show that restoration fidelity and task completion are insufficient measures of recovery correctness. A system can restore bytes accurately but choose a disallowed source. It can also finish the task while hiding the fact that it resumed from unsupported progress. Progress controls are used to attribute retained work to checkpoint restoration rather than repeated execution. Authorization tests distinguish between enforcing a recovery decision at runtime and merely checking a policy afterward.","reading_guide":"Skim in about 20 to 30 minutes. Start with the document-editing example in the introduction; it gives the clearest motivation for why the newest checkpoint may be the wrong continuation source. Then read the problem formulation and the definitions of continuation source, recovery route, and continuation authority. The most useful part is the experimental design, because it turns recovery errors into observable system-level failures rather than relying on final task success.","authors_note":""},"recommendation":{"zh":"读抽象和实验设计即可；它把 Agent checkpoint 从工程细节提升为可验证契约，这个 prior 值得更新。","en":"Read the abstraction and experiment design; it usefully reframes agent checkpointing as a verifiable contract rather than a storage detail."},"metadata":{"institutions":[],"authors":["Zhihui Zhang","Wei Liu"],"notable_authors":[],"tags":["long-horizon-agent","recoverability","checkpointing","agent-systems","reliability"],"links":{"arxiv":"https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.13672","pdf":"https://arxiv.org/pdf/2609.13672","github":null,"project":null,"source_url":null,"article":null}}},{"id":"2609.15818","title":"Atria Dawn: The Dawn of Agentic Superintelligence","abstract":"As AI agents become participants in the development of their successors, they reshape both the production of intelligence and the role of human researchers. We introduce Atria Dawn Preview, a foundation agentic language model designed for scientific research and engineering workflows, with the goal of expanding the frontier of agent productivity in the real world. This model is trained via a Verifiable Experience Pipeline that connects tool-mediated interactions to executable environments and ex","starred":false,"priority":"p2","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T16:22:30Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15818.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作要解决的不是单点 agent 能力，而是一个更难的问题：怎样把“可验证的工具交互经验”系统化地变成 foundation agent 的通用能力，并进一步观察这种 agent 在真实研发流程里到底承担了什么角色。论文把问题拆成两层。第一层是训练层：普通静态文本预训练很难覆盖长程工具使用、环境反馈、失败恢复、产物校验这些行为；如果没有外部可验证信号，agent 学到的往往只是表面格式，而不是可复用的工作流能力。第二层是组织层：当 agent 开始参与模型研发本身，单看 benchmark 分数已经不够，需要知道它是在提出方法、执行实验、诊断错误，还是只是在做低层劳动。Atria Dawn 试图同时回答这两层问题。","importance":"对基座研究的意义主要在训练信号设计，而不在单个 benchmark 排名。它给出的核心贡献是一个 Verifiable Experience Pipeline：把任务、轨迹、工具调用、生成产物和外部验证证据绑定起来，再把这些经验回流到 agentic foundation model 中。这个思路和传统 RLHF、纯 SFT、以及只靠 synthetic traces 的 agent 训练不同，重点是让训练样本带有“环境闭环”和“结果可核验”两种属性。对预训练/中训练研究员来说，真正有价值的地方有三点。第一，它把 post-train 常见的工具使用训练，往更接近持续预训练或 mid-training 的方向推了一步。第二，它强调失败样本不是简单丢弃，而是可作为诊断和任务扩展的来源，这和数据课程、active data construction、continual pretrain 的思路相通。第三，它把 agent 研发过程本身当作研究对象，给出人机分工的实证记录，这对理解未来“模型参与模型开发”的训练闭环有参考价值。量级上，这更像一个范式性工程系统展示，而不是一个已经被充分拆解、可直接复现的训练 recipe。","technical_approach":"方法主体建立在一个 744B 参数的 MoE foundation model 之上，但论文真正的技术重点不在底座架构，而在经验构造与筛选流程。\n\n第一步是把训练任务接到真实执行环境。模型不是只看 instruction 和答案，而是观察环境状态、调用工具、读取工具输出、生成中间产物，并根据反馈继续修正。这里覆盖的验证信号是多样的，包括可执行测试、实验指标、文件状态、几何结构检查、来源证据支持以及人工定义标准。这样做的目的，是把“是否完成任务”从语言表面判断，转成外部世界中的可验证结果。\n\n第二步是记录完整经验单元。一个训练单元不只是 prompt-response，而是 task + trajectory + artifacts + verification evidence 的绑定结构。这个设计比只保留成功 CoT 更强，因为它保留了行为链条和结果依据，便于模型学习何时检查状态、何时调用工具、何时回退重试。\n\n第三步是轨迹清洗与行为有效性过滤。论文明确提到会去掉不完整、互相矛盾、重复、或行为上无效的样本。这里的关键不是常规去重，而是“behaviorally invalid examples”的过滤，也就是即便文本看起来合理，但如果与环境状态、产物或验证证据不一致，也不进入训练集。这一点和普通合成数据过滤有明显区别。\n\n第四步是 failure-driven task construction。作者不是把失败仅仅当作负例，而是把 recurring problems 归纳成新的任务构造和环境改进来源，例如工具选择失效、验证不充分、证据缺失、恢复失败等。对长工作流，还会显式记录启动作业、查看日志和产物、修订执行计划等过程，目的是教会模型形成可迁移的调试与恢复策略。\n\n从方法定位看，它不是提出一个新的 RL 算法，也不是标准的 outcome reward optimization。更接近“以外部验证为锚点的数据引擎 + agentic mid-train/post-train 混合流程”。和只做结果监督相比，它保留了过程；和只做过程蒸馏相比，它要求过程能被外部结果约束；和纯在线 RL 相比，它更强调经验构造、筛选和复用。","experiments":"实验部分分成两块：一块是 16 个 agent benchmark 的能力评估，另一块是 Atria Dawn 自身研发过程的人机协作分析。\n\n能力评估上，论文称 Atria Dawn Preview 在 16 个基准上测试，覆盖 tool use、search/research、workspace productivity、professional tasks、software engineering、terminal tasks、ML engineering、cybersecurity 等类别；在其中 5 个 benchmark 上拿到已报告最好成绩，在另外 3 个上排第二。全文给出的信息更偏结果概览，当前可见内容没有完整展开每个 benchmark 的具体分数、方差、成本和运行配置，因此很难像标准学术论文那样精确判断提升来自底座模型、训练数据规模、工具接口设计，还是评测 protocol 差异。能确认的是：作者刻意强调“不同领域表现并不均匀”，这说明模型不是在所有 agent 任务上都稳定占优。\n\n更有信息量的是研发过程分析。作者收集了 56 名参与者、769 条任务记录，并结合 agent logs 分析人机分工。一个关键数字是：参与者认为大约三分之一已完成的 AI-assisted 任务，在相同范围和资源约束下，如果没有 AI 将不可行。论文还区分了更细粒度角色，例如谁提出方法、谁做选择、谁诊断困难、谁执行修改。结论是 agent 经常负责提出候选方案和执行修改，而人更多集中在评估、选择和方向控制。这部分不是传统 benchmark，但对 agent 训练研究更有启发，因为它揭示了当前 agent 更擅长“展开搜索和执行”，而不是“最终研究判断”。\n\n从实验不足看，论文目前公开内容对训练数据规模、经验条目数量、成功/失败比例、不同验证信号占比、以及各类过滤规则的贡献拆解还不够。若想把它转化成可复用 recipe，这些消融是最需要补的。","reading_guide":"这篇更适合带着问题去读，而不是逐段细抠所有案例。\n\n第一优先级看 Introduction 和 Atria Dawn Preview 部分，抓住作者到底把什么当作训练信号。重点不是“744B MoE”这个底座，而是 task-trajectory-artifact-verification 这四元绑定，以及为什么要把外部验证证据保留下来。\n\n第二优先级看 Verifiable Experience Pipeline 的流程描述。建议重点找三类细节：一是哪些环境反馈被当作监督；二是哪些轨迹会被判为 behaviorally invalid；三是失败案例如何反过来生成新任务和新检查项。这三点决定它到底是数据工程包装，还是有方法论含量的数据闭环。\n\n第三优先级看 benchmark 的前提条件。\n\n如果时间有限，30-40 分钟可以完成泛读；如果想提炼成训练 recipe，需要额外追踪附录、官网 benchmark 细节和可能的系统卡片。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper is not mainly about pushing a single agent benchmark. Explicit Failure Attribution and Structured Repair for Interactive Language Agents","abstract":"Language agents increasingly rely on reusable skills, but post-failure repair is often handled by opaque one-shot reflection: a model generates a skill patch without explicitly maintaining how failure explanations relate to candidate repairs or how unsuccessful retests should influence later edits. We introduce RESKILL, a structured repair framework that maintains an explicit repair state across repair rounds. Given a failed rollout, the framework links failure hypotheses to candidate skill agent 在失败后的“技能修复”问题。难点不在于让模型生成一个补丁，而在于失败轨迹通常支持多个可能原因，补丁又只覆盖其中一部分；如果系统不显式记录“哪个补丁在修哪个假设”，后续 retest 失败时就很难做 repair credit assignment，容易反复围绕错误诊断打转。","importance":"它和基座预训练的直接关系不强，更接近 agent post-training / test-time improvement。但有一个值得记住的启发：当模型输出的不只是答案，而是可复用技能或外部程序时，失败后的改进需要显式状态，而不是一次性 reflection。这个思路对持续学习、外部记忆驱动的自我修复、以及训练后阶段如何积累高质量改进信号有参考价值。贡献量级属于结构化 agent repair 框架，不是新的基础模型范式。","technical_approach":"作者提出 ReSkill，把失败后修复拆成几个显式步骤。先用冻结的语言模型基于失败轨迹生成一组 failure hypotheses、candidate patches，以及一个 coverage matrix，表示每个 patch 覆盖哪些假设。然后算法维护当前活跃假设的权重，根据加权覆盖度选择一个局部补丁，应用到外部 skill set 上，再把修改后的技能集放回环境里 retest。如果 retest 仍失败，就用新的失败轨迹更新假设和权重，进入下一轮。\n\n关键点有两个。第一，模型参数始终冻结，修改对象是外部技能描述，因此 repair 是可追踪、可回滚、可局部替换的。第二，repair state 是跨轮维护的，而不是每轮都把历史压缩进一句自然语言反思里。这样做的好处是把 diagnosis、patch generation、patch selection、retest feedback 四件事拆开，减少单次生成把几种推理混在一起的噪声。\n\n和 Reflexion 一类 one-shot reflection 相比，ReSkill 更像一个带显式信用分配的搜索/编辑过程。它不是让模型“再想一遍”，而是要求模型给出结构化修复因子，再由外层过程根据覆盖关系和环境反馈做选择。","experiments":"实验在两个交互环境上做：ALFWorld 的具身家庭任务，以及 AgentGym 里的 TextCraft 符号 crafting 任务；合起来形成六个 benchmark–model 设置。作者报告，在固定 repair budget 下，ReSkill 相比两个有竞争力的 repair baseline，最终修复成功率平均分别提升 3.7 和 3.3 个百分点。这个提升不算大，但在多轮修复、预算受限的设定里是有意义的，说明显式 attribution 和 retest-conditioned update 确实减少了无效补丁。\n\n从文中描述看，收益主要来自两类场景：一类是失败原因不止一个，单个 patch 只能部分覆盖；另一类是第一次修补后暴露出新的失败模式，需要根据 retest 结果重排假设优先级。作者也强调方法不改模型权重，因此结果更能说明 repair procedure 本身的作用，而不是额外训练带来的能力提升。\n\n局限也比较清楚：增益幅度中等，评测环境仍是相对受控的文本/具身基准；是否能迁移到更开放的工具使用、代码 agent repair trace 分析，因为后者更能说明结构化状态到底帮了什么。\n\n如果只留 20 分钟，建议读方法总览、patch selection
