This paper identifies the mechanism. Reflexion-style agents already detect dangerous plan steps through iterative self-critique, yet the architecture provides no pathway from detection to action. We call this the enforcement gap: the audit sees the problem; the controller ignores","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T09:46:59Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15293.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇论文讨论的是 Reflexion/ReAct 类 LLM Agent 的一个控制流缺陷：模型在自我反思阶段已经识别出危险计划，但执行控制器没有把审计结果作为硬约束，只把它当日志或建议，最后仍然执行原计划。","importance":"对预训练方向的直接贡献有限，但对 Agent 训练和评测有一个清晰提醒：安全能力不能只看模型是否能“说出风险”，还要看系统是否把风险信号接入执行路径。这个区分对后训练也有意义，因为很多 RL/SFT 训练会优化审计、反思、解释能力，但如果执行架构没有 enforcement，检测能力不会转化为实际安全性。","technical_approach":"论文把 Agent 安全链路拆成两个变量：检测概率 p_d，即审计器是否发现注入或危险步骤；执行概率 p_e，即控制器是否在审计器报警后停止执行。作者指出，已有框架通常把 p_d 和 p_e 混在端到端攻击成功率里，导致问题被掩盖。方法上，他们在 plan → reflect → replan 的循环中加入一个简单的 abort-on-flag 原语：如果反思/审计输出停止信号，控制器必须中止，而不是继续重规划或执行。论文还区分了三类失败：enforcement failure、detection failure、audit ambiguity。前者是控制流问题，后两者分别对应审计器漏检和审计结果不可解析。对于 ambiguity，作者还提到用 GRPO 训练 enforcement controller 来稳定解析审计输出。","experiments":"关键结果是：默认主流 Agent 框架中，检测率 p_d 仍能保持在 68% 以上，但执行概率 p_e 近似为 0，因此端到端攻击成功率仍然很高。加入一个少于 20 行代码的 abort-on-flag 检查后，聚合攻击成功率下降超过 4 倍。实验覆盖五个 frontier model、五个主要 Agent 框架，并在一个独立 benchmark 上复验。论文还比较了两类攻击面：plan-phase injection 是主要风险；observation-stream injection 在附录实验中 ASR 为 1.8%，说明工具输出注入在该设定下弱于计划阶段注入。开放权重模型的 audit leak rate 相差约 100 倍，说明检测端仍然存在模型差异。","reading_guide":"建议泛读 Introduction 和 Background/Theory，重点看 p_d / p_e 的拆分、Proposition 1，以及 §4.1 中开启 enforcement 前后的 ASR 对比。防御结构排序、blind-subspace protocol、GRPO enforcement controller 可以快速扫读。预计阅读 25–35 分钟。真正有用的点不是“加一个 if 判断”，而是把 Agent 安全评测从端到端 ASR 拆成检测、解析、执行三个可定位环节。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets a control-flow failure in Reflexion-style LLM agents: the reflection module can flag unsafe plan steps, but the controller treats the verdict as advisory text and proceeds with execution.","importance":"Its main value for foundation-model researchers is not a new pretraining method. It is a clean systems lesson for agent training and evaluation: eliciting a safety judgment from the model is not the same as enforcing it. This matters for post-training pipelines that optimize critique, reflection, or self-monitoring; those signals only help if they are wired into the action path.","technical_approach":"The authors decompose agent safety into detection probability p_d and enforcement probability p_e. p_d measures whether the audit flags the malicious or unsafe step; p_e measures whether the controller halts when a flag appears. Existing end-to-end ASR hides this distinction. The proposed fix is an abort-on-flag primitive in the plan-reflect-replan loop, making the audit verdict binding. The paper further organizes failures into enforcement failure, detection failure, and audit ambiguity. For ambiguous critiques, it reports a GRPO-trained enforcement controller that maps uncertain audit text into executable stop/continue decisions.","experiments":"The reported measurements show that default frameworks often retain non-trivial detection ability, with p_d above 68%, while p_e is effectively zero. Adding the binding enforcement check reduces aggregate attack success by more than 4x. The experiments span five frontier models, five major agent frameworks, and an independent benchmark. The paper also separates attack surfaces: plan-phase injection is the dominant setting, while observation-stream injection reaches 1.8% ASR in the appendix. Audit leak rates differ by roughly 100x across open-weight models, so model-side detection quality is still a real variable.","reading_guide":"Skim the Introduction and Background/Theory first, especially the p_d / p_e decomposition and Proposition 1. Then read §4.1 for the direct enforcement ablation. The defense ranking, blind-subspace protocol, and GRPO enforcement controller can be read selectively. Estimated reading time: 25–35 minutes. Existing skill self-evolution methods directly revise skill text based on execution feedback, but each oracle evaluation requires a full agent rollout, creating a supervision bottleneck that confines search to failure-patching updates. Our key insight is that ranking is a smoother supervision target than absolute outcome regression: identifying which skill is better requir","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-14T11:30:23Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_15396.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.AI"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇工作处理的是 agent skill 自演化里的监督稀缺问题。现有方法把 skill 文本直接当优化对象，每次改完都要跑一次完整 agent rollout，再用 oracle 的成败或分数回传信号。问题在于，这种监督既贵又稀：一次评估要消耗整条执行轨迹，skill 文本空间又高度离散，少量 noisy 的 outcome 信号很难稳定指向“下一版 skill 应该怎么改”。结果就是优化容易退化成局部补丁，只修最近一次失败案例，难以形成可迁移的高质量技能。","importance":"它对基座预训练本身不是直接贡献，但对 post-train 阶段的外部记忆/外部策略优化有现实启发，尤其是“昂贵环境反馈如何被摊薄成可扩展监督”这个问题。更具体地说，这篇文章把 reward modeling / preference learning 里的一个老思路搬到了 skill evolution：不要直接拟合绝对分数，而是先学一个与 oracle 排序对齐的中间评价空间。这个思路对 RLHF、verifier-based training、tool-use policy refinement、甚至合成 curriculum 的设计都有借鉴意义，因为它强调的是如何把高成本评估转成低成本、可复用、可解释的 dense signal。","technical_approach":"方法核心是 SkillLift，把 skill evolution 写成一个双层优化问题。上层目标不是直接优化 skill，而是让一个“rubric”对 skill 的排序尽量贴近 oracle 排序；下层则固定 rubric，用 rubric 产生的 dense feedback 去更新 skill。作者的关键判断是：排序比绝对打分更平滑，也更省 oracle 调用，因为只需要知道 A 是否优于 B，而不必精确预测每个 skill 的绝对质量。\n\n具体做法分两步交替进行。第一步是 skill-update：固定当前 rubric，把它当作廉价 surrogate evaluator，对一组候选 skill 给出结构化反馈，再据此生成改写后的 skill。这里的收益不是只得到一个 scalar，而是得到更细粒度、可解释的评价维度，因此 skill 更新不必围绕单次失败样本打补丁。第二步是 rubric-update：固定 skill population，只在必要时调用 oracle，对候选 skill 做真实评估，并用这些结果去校准 rubric 诱导出的排序，使 rubric 继续贴近 oracle 偏好。\n\n从复杂度上，作者强调其 oracle 调用复杂度从很多现有方法的 O(LT) 降到 O(L)，其中 L 是演化轮数，T 是每轮内部优化 epoch。也就是说，内部大量搜索和比较尽量在 surrogate 空间里完成，oracle 只负责周期性重对齐。文章还把这一点和已有方法做了表格对比：有些方法没有 surrogate，有些有 surrogate 但没有 dense feedback，SkillLift 试图同时拿到“可演化 + surrogate + dense feedback + 低 oracle 复杂度”这四点。\n\n从方法味道上看，它更接近 preference-model-guided search，而不是直接 outcome regression。与直接学 reward model 的区别在于，作者把中间对象显式设计成 rubric，也就是结构化评价空间，而不是单一黑盒分数器。这让它更适合驱动文本 skill 的编辑，因为编辑器可以围绕 rubric 维度做定向修改。","experiments":"文中先给了一个 pilot study，基于 10 个 WildClawBench 任务、10 轮演化、每个 skill 3 个 rollout seed。这个实验主要不是为了刷分，而是证明现有 direct optimization 的瓶颈：best/mean/worst skill score 的差距长期很大，说明方差高、优化不稳；同时 oracle token 消耗远高于框架本身开销，而且随轮数持续增长。\n\n正式实验覆盖两个复杂 agent benchmark。摘要里给出的主结果是：在相同设置下，SkillLift 优于现有 auto-skill self-evolving 方法；即便给 baseline 2 倍更大的 token budget，SkillLift 仍然更强；达到同等目标性能时，token cost 可降低 40% 到 70%。这组结果的意义不只是“更省 token”，而是说明把 oracle 反馈先蒸馏成排序一致的 rubric，确实能改变搜索效率曲线。\n\n从消融逻辑看，最值得注意的不是某个单点数字，而是三件事：一，ranking target 是否比 absolute score 更稳；二，alternating optimization 是否比直接端到端修 skill 更有效；三，dense rubric feedback 是否真的减少了 failure-patching。文章的叙述表明，这三点都得到了支持。若看全文，建议特别留意作者是否报告了 rubric 与 oracle 排序一致性的提升、不同 oracle 调用频率下的性能退化、以及 skill population 多样性是否因此更好保留。这些比最终平均分更能说明方法是否可扩展。","reading_guide":"泛读可以抓三部分。先看 Introduction 里的 Figure 1 和 Table 1，作者想解决什么、为什么现有方法扩不动，基本都在这里。再看 Method 里双层优化和 alternating update 的定义，重点理解“为什么学排序一致的 rubric，而不是直接学 outcome predictor”。最后看实验部分关于 token budget、oracle 调用次数和 target performance cost 的对比，这决定了方法是否只是换了个表述，还是确实改善了 sample efficiency。\n\n如果时间有限，30 到 40 分钟足够把主线读清。若要深一点，建议盯住两个问题：rubric 的表示形式到底有多结构化，是否真能提供可操作的编辑反馈；以及 oracle 重对齐的频率如何选，过稀会不会导致 surrogate 漂移。这两个点决定它能否从论文设定走向更一般的 agent continual improvement。","authors_note":""},"summary_en":{"core_problem":"The paper targets the supervision bottleneck in skill self-evolution for LLM agents. Existing methods directly optimize the skill text using execution outcomes, but every oracle label requires a full agent rollout. That makes supervision expensive, sparse, and noisy. Since the search space is discrete text, a small number of rollout-level signals tends to produce conservative, failure-specific edits rather than stable improvement of the skill itself.","importance":"This is not a pretraining paper in the narrow sense, but it is relevant to the pretrain–posttrain interface. The useful idea is how to amortize expensive environment feedback into a reusable dense signal. The paper imports a preference-learning style solution into skill evolution: learn an oracle-aligned ranking space first, then optimize skills inside that space. That framing is potentially useful for verifier-based training, tool-use policy refinement, external memory optimization, and any setting where direct rollout supervision is too costly to support broad search.","technical_approach":"$86","experiments":"$87","reading_guide":"For a quick read, start with Figure 1 and Table 1 in the introduction. They make the bottleneck and the intended design trade-off very clear. Then read the method section carefully enough to understand the bilevel objective and the alternating updates. The key question is why oracle-aligned ranking should be easier to learn and more useful for editing than direct outcome prediction. Finally, inspect the experiments that compare token budget, oracle calls, and cost to reach a target score.\n\nA 30 to 40 minute skim is enough to capture the main idea. For a deeper read, focus on two issues: how structured the rubric representation really is, and how often oracle realignment is needed before the surrogate starts drifting. languag","starred":false,"priority":"p3","reading_tier":"skim","source":"arxiv","sources":["arxiv"],"published_at":"2026-09-11T09:48:43Z","category_id":"agent","cover_url":"/covers/2609_13334.png","hf_upvotes":0,"arxiv_categories":["cs.SE","cs.AI","cs.MA"],"summary":{"core_problem":"这篇 coupling：作者把持续部署拆成 action/skill/policy 三个改进闭环，认为只有共享同一 substrate，这些闭环的改进才能复利式叠加；否则每个环在不同表示上优化，难以积累。\n在落地层面，文中给了一个四层框架（Data/Knowledge/Intelligence/Governance），并引入 per-skill trust operation.
